## §12.8 — Relationship to Task D

**ECB feeds Task D. It does not become Task D.** `_06 §6.5` defers the incumbent-composition contest, copyability adjudication and the three Family-J falsifiers to Task D under a scope rule, and Task D has its own governing method, comparator modes, H0–H3 alternatives and admission logic, with an interim terminus that has issued no final verdict.

**So:** ECB conducts the comparisons its own decisions require, and records results in a form Task D can consume. **It does not assume Task D's scope, close its H0–H3 adjudication, or issue its admission verdict.** Neither posture is acceptable: deferring every serious rival to Task D would leave ECB's own choices untested, and absorbing Task D would import another programme's unfinished obligations and gate authority.

**Where an ECB result addresses a Task-D obligation, record the correspondence** — requirement or fixture · assumptions · candidate version · evaluation method · result · evidence level · remaining limitation — so Task D can accept the work rather than rerun it. **Reuse crosses the boundary; acceptance authority does not.** **The relationship must be resolved against** `v4_pre_spine_sufficiency_and_task_d_reentry_map_2026-08-04.md` and the interim terminus `v4_C4_2A_task_d_interim_closeout_and_pause.md` rather than invented — **and it has not been: this seat has not read either beyond confirming they exist** (`§13.2`). The map separates readiness and sequence from the scoring method and checkpoint state; **naming it does not reconcile it**, and that reconciliation is a Pass-1 obligation.

## §12.9 — THE EXECUTION CONTRACT *(binds every agent entering or continuing ECB)*

**This section states the scoped method ECB inherits, so that a replacement does not have to derive one mid-arc.** *(R22 withdrew the quantified claim that FAI *"reinvented roughly a quarter of its machinery"*; R23 withdraws the remaining categorical diagnosis that FAI *"reached Output 4 before it had a working method"* — the execution contract depends on **neither**, and attributed history belongs in `§16`. No retrospective FAI investigation is opened.)* The inheritance below is taken from mechanisms that already exist and were paid for; ECB adopts their behaviour without promoting FAI's architecture conclusions.

### §12.9.1 — Who this binds, and what a successor inherits

**This contract applies to every author and reviewer entering or continuing ECB — including replacement, restarted and compacted contexts.** A successor inherits the work object and its obligations, **not the predecessor's claimed familiarity, confidence, or unrecorded reading.** *(Agent Work Protocol `§2.1`: replacement assumes the same lane after explicit ownership transfer and freshness check; it creates no new lane and no new authority.)*

At fresh entry, or on loss of reliable working context, the agent recovers these facts **from the carrier, never from chat**:

| Required entry fact | Durable home |
|---|---|
| Accepted plan baseline, or that G0 remains unaccepted | `§15.0` acceptance receipt + `§1` |
| Current head/blob, active writer, phase, bounded work item | `§1` operational state |
| Applicable execution and evaluation rules | `§§11`–`§13` |
| Current integrated account, and its proposal-vs-acceptance boundary | the G1 output sections at `§12.9.6` |
| Sources actually consumed, at what depth, with what remaining | `§13.2` live control |
| Unresolved findings, review instructions, exact next permitted action | current review/stop receipt |

**Baseline versus working carrier.** Ordinary authorized authoring moves the working head; **it does not silently move the accepted method.** A material change to scope, evaluation rules, acceptance conditions or authority limits requires explicit review. Unrelated settled content is not reopened for it.

**Proportionality.** A continuing agent with intact context need not reread the packet before every message. **A replacement, or a materially stale context, re-establishes the contract and current state before asserting anything.** Each agent reads the native sources for the source-derived claims **it personally asserts**; a predecessor's receipt is attributable evidence, not personal consumption (`§13.0` ①/②).

**Stale-pointer exception, stated so it is not rediscovered every entry.** `main`'s boot pointer names the pre-FAI checkpoint. **ECB's assignment and the pinned FAI pause context govern this work — that context being `.cursor/plans/HANDOFF_2026-08-23_fai_g1_paused_by_operator.md` @ `65f310e0b40901cb4f34bb607d92b37ecf9be043`, on branch `cursor/fai-g1-operating-model-4933` (PR #17), which is present on neither `main` nor this branch. The older pointer does not redirect the agent to Insurance, and no pointer-cleanup project is authorized.**

### §12.9.2 — Work units: a phase label is not authority to finish the phase

`RECONCILE → TRACE → EXTRACT` are phases, **not three agent responses.**

> **Each authorized work unit names one substantive question, its required source packet, the carrier sections it may update, its expected result, and its review stop. It ends when that result is ready — or earlier, when unresolved semantic load requires a checkpoint (`§13.0` ③). No agent may treat a phase label as blanket authority to complete the phase.**

| Phase | Work | Required result before release |
|---|---|---|
| **RECONCILE — unit 1** | recover the inherited joint care/business account at its actual scope | attributed account; accepted-vs-candidate distinctions; contradictions; reusable evidence; named proof debt |
| **RECONCILE — later units** | reconcile remaining decision-relevant source families incl. native owners and forensic corrections; develop alternatives where the question needs them | a coherent **comparative candidate account**, not a source list; **no undisclosed material source gap behind a retained claim** |
| **TRACE — preparation** | instantiate `§12.9.4` against recovered candidates and selected inherited/new cases | reviewable case inputs, candidate versions, expected predicates, evidence basis, coverage and known limits — **before scoring** |
| **TRACE — evaluation units** | run declared traces, apply controls, challenge coverage, revise candidates where warranted | recorded results and counterexamples; **materially affected tests rerun after any candidate change**; independent challenge dispositioned |
| **EXTRACT** | consolidate the operating account; produce A–D | authorable downstream package; evidence-backed decision dispositions |
| **G2** | adjudicate the assembled result and its limits | acceptance, amendment, rejection, or bounded insufficiency **at the evidence level actually earned** |

**No unit completion is self-acceptance.** Nick releases the next bounded work after review. **Inside its scope the author reads, reasons, invents alternatives, challenges sources and repairs the artifact without asking permission for each step.**

### §12.9.3 — The first activated work unit, specified now

**Question.** What does the inherited joint care/business account already say about how **care resolution, sourcing, fulfillment, settlement and independently governed participation** compose — and which parts are accepted at scope, which are candidate, and which are still owed?

**Entry packet.** The `EVRUN-2026-000012` directory, entering through **`_07`** as the disposition and consumption endpoint, reading **`_02 §15`** as the operative structural account and **`_04`** as the controlling Gate-3 adjudication.

> **Derivation order, so the unit does not manufacture false corroboration in its first act.** `_04` is **upstream**: `_02 §15`'s own header names `_04` as the controlling adjudication, and `_04` L13 states *"Opus's integration lives in `_02 §15`."* **`_02 §15` integrates `_04`'s rulings, so their agreement is NOT independent corroboration** — **but neither is it guaranteed: faithful integration still has to be checked, and this review round is itself proof that an instruction and the artifact claiming to implement it can diverge.** **Treat concordance as derivational, and verify the integration rather than assuming it** (`§13.0` ②; `H-CIRC`). **The independent layer is `_03`**, the frozen unblinded adjudication `_04` weighed — and **`_01`** is the frozen blind alternative decomposition produced without OMNI context. **Follow `_07` into any further native section this unit's claims materially rely on; do not substitute `_07` for those bodies.**

**Write result.** The initial inherited account in `§12.9.6`'s designated section; source-to-consequence rows in `§13.2`; named proof debts and the next source cohort. **No architecture acceptance. No claim the source floor is complete. No frontier-sufficiency verdict.**

**Stop.** Return for review before opening the next substantial cohort — **or earlier if source or relationship conflicts cannot be responsibly synthesized in the current context.**

**This is the first unit. It is not the reconciliation phase, and it is not an instruction to rebuild `EVRUN-000012`.**

### §12.9.4 — What running a test means

**Default: a source-grounded, stepwise design trace.** Any use of an executable model or observed evidence is labelled distinctly. **Prose execution, simulation, implementation and deployed proof are four different things and are never reported as one.**

Each scored case carries: **question and competing accounts** (which decision it tests; exact candidate versions; **the exact candidate clauses each predicate depends on** — so a clause change yields the affected-test set by lookup rather than recall, which is what makes the rerun rule observable rather than assertive. **Lookup returns the RECORDED dependencies only: the change review must still consider newly exposed or previously unrecorded semantic effects, and this is not a claim of complete impact reach** (`H-IMPACT` is the open question, not a solved one); the strongest applicable inherited or simpler alternative) · **initial conditions** (participants, responsibilities, authority assumptions, resources, evidence, versions, timing, **and what each participant can actually observe**) · **event sequence** (requests, responses, delays, changes, refusals, partial effects, failures, in explicit order) · **expected behaviour** (predicates fixed **before** scoring, each with the source or declared design requirement supporting it; assumed clinical or scientific conditions separated from verified ones) · **trace** (per consequential step: responsible owner, information used, permission, state change, external effect, uncertainty, continuing obligation) · **result and limitation** (satisfied · concrete violation · underspecified · unsupported assumption · insufficient evidence) · **consequence** (which candidate clause or downstream obligation changes; which prior tests are affected; what needs independent checking).

**The evaluator may not repair an underspecified candidate mid-trace and then score the repaired behaviour as if it had been specified.**

**Expected behaviour needs an attributable basis. Reviewer agreement establishes no biological efficacy, no control-system safety, and no institutional acceptance.** Where those facts are unavailable the test may examine whether the arrangement **preserves the uncertainty and routes the decision correctly** — it cannot award the missing external proof.

**Comparison uses the same demanded outcome, operating span and material conditions.** A whole OMNI arrangement is never compared against a competitor artificially stopped at storage, publication or messaging. **Value evaluation counts work shifted onto patients, clinicians, counterparties and operators**; analytical estimates and synthetic traces stay labelled and produce no measured benefit.

**A method defect discovered mid-evaluation is recorded and reviewed before affected scoring continues; unaffected work does not automatically stop.**

### §12.9.5 — Two test specifications, prepared now and deliberately unscored

**These exist to prove the method is usable before G1 begins. Candidate-specific results stay empty until the declared evaluation stage.**

#### SPEC-ORD — ordinary care, external fulfillment

**Inputs.** Patient `P`; valid authorized prescription `Rx-1` with a recorded prescriber authority basis effective from `T0`. **The post-`T6` act under test is issuing the authorized replacement request to `Y` — NOT fulfillment, and not `Y`'s acceptance of it.** **Declared minimum action-critical set for that act** *(a floor the candidate must meet, supplied so `P5` is decidable in both directions)*: `{ the current adopted instruction and its version lineage back to Rx-1 · the applicable prescriber authority basis, still effective · patient identity binding intact · the declared eligibility determination for the substituted product · an honest representation of Y's acceptance state }`.

> **Three things this set deliberately does not do.** It does **not** require `Rx-1` itself to remain unsuperseded — **a successor instruction linked to the original, carrying valid current authority, is admissible**; the fixture has not specified in-place modification as the required representation and must not exclude versioned replacement by accident. It does **not** require `Y`'s acceptance to be affirmative — **`Y`'s acceptance state must be observable and honestly represented, and unknown or not-yet-accepted must be mistaken for neither acceptance nor a prohibition on sending the request.** And it is **a floor, not a ceiling: additional checks with a stated basis are not failures.** `T-15` requires *minimum-sufficient* revalidation under the governing contract; it establishes no unconditional prohibition on justified further verification. **This set is the fixture's declared assumption, not a derived universal limit on verification.** independently governed pharmacy `Y` — a counterparty with **no delegated command to OMNI** and its own native record. **Observable to OMNI:** only what `Y` transmits. **Observable to `Y`:** what OMNI transmits, plus its own stock.

**Event sequence.** `T1` OMNI transmits the order · `T2` `Y` returns a transport-level acknowledgment · `T3` silence — no substantive acceptance · `T4` a **duplicate** acknowledgment arrives carrying the **same source event identity** as `T2` · `T5` `Y` responds proposing substitution of product `B` for `A`, citing stock · `T6` the prescriber reviews and adopts or declines.

**Expected predicates, each with its provenance.** **P1** the `T2` acknowledgment does not set accepted-custody state *(`D0W3B-GRD-002`; C4.3 ten-state ladder)*. **P2** `T4` resolves as a **redelivery of the `T2` source event, not a second independent assertion** — **decidable rule: same source event identity ⇒ deduplicate; a distinct source assertion ⇒ two events**, and the case states which it is *(Care `§9a` multiplicity ≠ independence)*. **P3** between `T2` and `T5` a candidate may advance readiness and logistics state but **may not** set fulfillment-accepted or close the care obligation *(release is state, not authority — Care forensic `§G`)*. **P4** the `T5` substitution is a **counterparty assertion, not an adopted clinical change**; adoption requires the `T6` decision *(Observation `§4` **three distinct gates** — *"'verification' is NOT one word (Nick + Knox locked — DO NOT collapse)"*; release ≠ adoption)*. **P5** after `T6` adoption, the replacement request proceeds **only on revalidation of at least the declared minimum action-critical set** *(C4.5 `T-15`)*. **Decidable in both directions: omitting any member of that set fails, and re-running the whole authorization as unnecessary full reconstruction also fails. An additional check carrying a stated basis is neither** — *the goal is less unnecessary reconstruction, never no verification* (`§3.3`).

**Positive path — and note what it may NOT assume.** Where `T6` is **adoption**, the demonstrated positive path ends at **an authorized replacement request to `Y`**. It does **not** reach completed fulfillment, because `Y`'s **own substantive acceptance and performance evidence are separately owned and have not been supplied by this sequence** — letting prescriber adoption stand in for the counterparty's commitment is the very substitution `P1` forbids. To demonstrate completion, the case must extend the sequence with `Y`'s acceptance and execution evidence. Where `T6` is **decline**, the positive path is the order resolving without the substitution, with the obligation correctly left open or closed on its own terms. **A candidate that can only block is a failure here; so is one that completes on the prescriber's signature alone.**

**Counterexamples — must fail.** Any candidate that sets accepted state at `T2` · counts `T4` as corroboration · treats `T5` as adopted · **or proceeds after `T6` without revalidating the declared minimum action-critical set.** **Must NOT fail:** a candidate representing the adoption as an authorized successor instruction linked to `Rx-1`; a candidate sending the request while `Y`'s acceptance state is honestly unknown; a candidate performing an extra check on a stated basis.

**Question each candidate must answer.** Which OMNI-side owner makes the decision consequent on `T6`.

**Limits.** No claim about `Y`'s internal correctness; no legal determination; **design trace only** (`§12.9.4`).

#### SPEC-GEN — generated intervention, irreversible preparation

**Inputs.** Patient `P`; design `D-v1` with a versioned basis `{specimen S drawn T0, interpretation I-v1, patient state Q-v1}`; manufacturing commitment `M` holding a slot; **an irreversible preparation step consuming specimen material**; administration planned for `T6`. **`D-v1`'s stated prerequisite set, supplied here so `P4` is decidable and both branches are reachable:** `PR-1` the target identified in `I-v1` is present · `PR-2` specimen `S` meets the declared viability threshold · `PR-3` no contraindicating co-treatment recorded as of release. **The versioned basis `{S, I-v1, Q-v1}` is the provenance of those prerequisites, not the prerequisite set itself** — a change to the basis does not automatically invalidate a prerequisite, which is exactly what `P4` decides.

**Event sequence.** `T1` `D-v1` released to manufacture on that basis · `T2` `M` accepts, slot held · `T3` irreversible preparation begins; material consumed · `T4` a material context change arrives — result `R` moves interpretation to `I-v2` *(variant: patient state to `Q-v2`)* · `T5` reconsideration · `T6` administer, withhold, or revise.

**Expected predicates, each with its provenance.** **P1** `M`'s completion establishes **manufacturing release, not current clinical suitability** *(release ≠ adoption)*. **P2** the `T1` decision basis is preserved unaltered and `R` enters as new evidence *(`REV-184` — outcome reads the frozen context, never rewrites it)*. **P3** consumed material is accounted as consumed; **no candidate may assume rollback of physical work**. **P4** **decidable rule: does `R` invalidate `PR-1`, `PR-2` or `PR-3`, or does it bear on something outside that set?** Run **both variants**: **(a)** `R` moves `PR-1` from satisfied to unsatisfied — invalidation · **(b)** `R` reports a finding outside `PR-1`–`PR-3` that a reasonable reviewer must weigh — reconsideration without invalidation. **Neither variant asserts a biological rule; they exist to prove the method can tell the two apart.** **P5** reconsideration routes to the **currently rightful authority for the affected decision** — **not to whoever detected `R`**, and **not to whoever was named at `T1` merely because they were named.** `T1`'s decision and authorship are preserved as **historical evidence**; the holder may have departed, lost authority or transferred it. **Yesterday's identity is not tomorrow's authorization**, which is the `H-SURV` failure this arc exists to examine. **P6** a **no-change** outcome is valid and must be representable.

**Positive path — and the distinction it must not collapse.** Where `R` does not invalidate a prerequisite *(variant b)*, **the design, the manufacturing work and the consumed material are preserved and nothing is restarted.** That is **not** the same as authorization to administer. **Proceeding additionally requires an affirmative current suitability decision by the currently rightful authority and satisfaction of the applicable execution conditions.** So variant (b) has **two correct outcomes and a candidate must be able to produce either**: proceed on an affirmative decision, **or a legitimate withhold pending clarification, which is a correct result and not a failure.** *"The prior design has not been invalidated" establishes neither "the current suitability decision is affirmative" nor "all action-critical conditions are satisfied."* **A candidate that can only block still fails; so does one that treats non-invalidation as permission.**

**Counterexample — must fail.** Any candidate that restarts everything on any change, treats the `T1` approval as sufficient at `T6`, or silently rewrites `I-v1`.

**Limits.** No clinical or scientific claim about the intervention; **the suitability judgment belongs to a human authority, not to the trace** (`§12.9.4`).

**Both remain unscored. Candidate-specific results stay empty until the declared evaluation stage.**

### §12.9.6 — Durable homes, so nothing dies in a thread

| Content | Home |
|---|---|
| current integrated operating account | `§7.4` *(created on first RECONCILE unit)* |
| evaluation specifications and results | `§11.6` *(created at TRACE preparation)* |
| value claim and evidence contract | Output C, `§12` |
| downstream dispositions | Output D, `§12` |
| live source-to-consequence control | `§13.2` |
| current remaining-work and review record | `§15.4` |
| **synthesis checkpoint** *(`§13.0` ③ — required when findings interact, and most likely invoked at the moment of interruption)* | **`§7.4`, as a dated sub-entry** — it had no declared home through R15 |
| historical lineage | `§16` — **and nothing current lives there** |

**A meaningful correction discovered inside a unit enters the relevant current section and its evidence record before that unit is returned. *"Discussed with Nick"* is not a preservation state.** Do not rewrite the whole dossier each round; update the affected account and preserve its lineage. **Do not leave a withdrawn assertion live because a correction appears elsewhere** — that failure has now occurred four times in this carrier.

### §12.9.7a — Coordination and snapshot controls *(referenced, not restated)*

**The governing rules already exist and ECB invokes them rather than copying them.** `doctrine/agent_work_protocol.md` **`§2.1`** — durable work identity, **one active writer per writable object and one per branch**, explicit replacement transfer with freshness check, the **Coordination Overlay** (L131) when coordination is genuinely material, and the **conditional base/reference mechanics** that avoid inventing pin commits where ancestry or an existing immutable reference already establishes the base. `doctrine/operator_context_and_collaboration_model.md` **`§§2.6`–`2.7`** — review-object identity, incremental and cumulative diff receipts, and **checking the intended recipient before processing a consequential relay.**

**ECB's actual default, stated once:** **one author writes this carrier** · reviewers inspect a **named snapshot** and return findings · the author records dispositions and makes the revisions · **writer replacement is explicit.** Multiple reviewers on one frozen object is normal and is not a coordination event.

**When a changed METHOD affects active work — FAI R8 `§4.4`, a dependency problem that flawless continuity does not solve.** **An object can have perfect handoff hygiene and still be evaluated under a method that changed underneath its author.** So when a method change materially influences active work, record in the existing state surfaces: **object version, writer and writable boundary · method version and change STATE · the trigger · the impact on active work · the required inheritance/freshness action · the next permitted act.** **Proposed, accepted, landed and CONSUMED-BY-THE-OBJECT are four different states and are not interchangeable.** **A lane returning on one trigger does not disposition another** — FAI `§4.18.5` records exactly that: a destination repair returned and inherited, while a separate reliance-generalization delta remained **relayed and unadopted.** At an accepted behavioural change the consuming object names the method version, controlling sections, exact delta and affected results at an explicit freshness boundary, **and no agent silently retrofits a new method to rescue a result under review.** **This is conditional record-keeping in existing surfaces — no new lane, registry, topology or method team, and it activates no paused FAI method candidate or PR #17.**

**Before any additional durable contribution is activated** — not merely thought about — it declares its **question · owner · exact input · owned output · writable boundary · dependency · return and disposition path.** **A second author does not acquire permission to edit this branch because both serve the same arc.** Shared-surface writes stay serialized. **Concurrency alone does not create a parent package, a common base, or an integrator** *(the 2026-08-09 work-horizon narrowing; standalone work needs no integrator)*. **No contribution or package is created by this paragraph.**

**Identify the writer by instance and bounded work item, not by the generic name "Opus."** Operational state lives once, in `§1`. **A frozen review receipt may repeat exact refs; it must never masquerade as current state.**

**The head-versus-content rule, restored** *(it existed in R2's `§2.1` receipt and was lost in R5's compression — the exact convention the operator remembers building)*: **a frozen object is verified by its content blob, never by branch-head equality.** The FAI pause handoff is the worked example — three recorded heads, one carrier blob `9835715e`, because additive reverts and state-only changes moved the branch while preserving the content. ***"The branch moved" and "the thing we reviewed changed" are different statements and neither establishes the other.*** Where current control state and a frozen input intentionally sit at different refs, read **state from the control ref and the input from its declared content ref**, and never present an old input snapshot's checkpoint as today's state.

### §12.9.7 — Replacement readiness, and the four review responsibilities

**Before the plan is called startable, the carrier must answer these without chat history:** what exact object am I working on · which method applies · what has been accepted · what is my next bounded question · what must I read · where do I write · what remains unresolved · what evidence do I return · where must I stop.

**Interrupted-work case, which the contract must survive.** A unit has consumed part of its source packet, recorded a provisional contradiction, and stopped before resolving it. **The replacement preserves that state, resumes at the named remaining read, and converts the partial result into neither an accepted finding nor a reason to start over.**

**A reconstruction performed by the sitting author is a self-check, not a cold-agent test.** A bounded read-only continuation rehearsal can supply the stronger evidence; it stays a G0 readiness check and **authorizes no G1 execution.**

**What these controls do and do not buy.** They make review, recovery and attribution **more reliable when followed**. They are **manual controls, not semantic enforcement**: a missing insight can leave a perfectly tidy diff, and no convention here detects a weak agent automatically. **Claiming otherwise would be the same overclaim this carrier keeps having to retract.**

**How author and reviewer divide the reading — FAI `§E15.17.5`, because both wrong answers are tempting.** **The division is of RESPONSIBILITY, not of ACCESS.** The author is primarily responsible for recovery, connection and synthesis; **the reviewer is independently responsible for adequacy.** **Each seat reads sufficiently for the source-derived claims IT personally asserts — another seat's report is attributable evidence and never its own reading.** **Either may open a source the other omitted or widen a reread; selective rereading is a COST DEFAULT, not a ceiling.** So neither *"only the author reads and the reviewer adjudicates its summary"* **nor** *"both reread every source in full every pass"* is the rule, **and no staffing pattern follows from this.** **Review must test relationships ACROSS sources and the source SELECTION itself — not only whether the quotations support the sentences chosen.** **The fourth responsibility exists because the first three never asked whether the ACCOUNT OF THE WORK was true:** check the return against the actual diff, the live recovery record, the remaining-read list and the claimed next step. **Stopping because context is full can be correct; describing that stop as completion is the defect.**

**Review, adopted as behaviour rather than headcount** *(Output-4 convention, not promoted doctrine)*: **source adequacy** · **relationship integrity** · **cold artifact coherence** · **fidelity of the reported working state** — the last asking whether the artifact, diff, source record, remaining work and reported next step actually agree.

---

## §13 — Method, sources, preservation

**§13.0 Inherited execution method — FAI R8 `§§4.1–4.3`, control ref `65f310e0b40901cb4f34bb607d92b37ecf9be043`.** This arc has spent nine revisions reinventing source discipline that already exists in durable form. **It is adopted here as identified method inheritance — not as permission to resume FAI, import its architecture conclusions, or activate the separately controlled PR #19 method.** It supersedes nothing in `§13.1`; it is the layer `§13.1` was groping toward.

**① The default is the whole source, and reduced depth is a claim carrying its own basis.** Admissible: something established *inside* the source once opened, or the source being genuinely derivative of material already consumed. **Inadmissible: title · banner · folder · section heading · catalog or registry row · grep hit · a prior agent's summary.** *R10 violated this and corrects it: the thesis file was selected by hit count (`§13.2`).*

**② A claim's authority is the layer it appears in, not the layer that cites it** — raw packet · reviewer layer · registry entry · carrier citation. Name the layer before a load-bearing claim rests on it. **And correcting a citation does not correct the conclusion the citation carried:** the downstream consequences — false independence, false novelty, wrong grain, wrong destination, maturity overclaim — are re-examined separately.

**③ The control variable is unresolved semantic load, not file count.** When findings begin interacting, or source authority, output ownership or promotion status becomes uncertain, **stop acquiring sources and record a synthesis checkpoint before opening another cohort.** Before retaining an architecture-changing finding, establish six things: **source identity and exact sections · authority, maturity and supersession posture · the native mechanism without OMNI adaptation · existing architecture coverage · rightful destination · material uninspected scope.** *This is the answer to an agent reading broadly, making dozens of coupled judgments, and announcing the whole thing finished.*

**④ Promotion hold — five distinct acts.** Reading a source · recovering its native mechanism · routing the finding · **promoting architecture** · **accepting architecture**. A recovery pass performs the first three. Durable language — *missing shared law · contradiction · deficiency* — is earned after the checks above, not while the interpretation is still moving.

**⑤ Enter a prior workstream at its current terminus and current-state receipt** — never the first grep hit, the oldest contract, the parent charter or the most recently dated file. The packet members do different jobs: the integrated artifact supplies composition, the adjudication record supplies dispositions and corrections, native carriers supply authority, later additions supply evidence and **never automatic supersession**. **Status is scope-specific:** *frozen* restricts mutation and self-promotion, *closed* closes a named question, *excluded from an authoring set* excludes from that set — **none means unreadable.**

**⑥ Claim-scoped reliance — FAI carrier `§§4.18.1`–`4.18.2` *(candidate general method, object-local in FAI; adopted here as ECB execution behaviour, NOT promoted as universal doctrine)*.** **Three questions are answered separately and are ALLOWED TO DISAGREE: what currently governs · what is best supported · what is implemented or observed.** **A declared posture is a CLAIM, not force** (`§4.18.1` verbatim). **The obligation scales with the use, and these are different tasks:** **relying on a proposition as GOVERNING** requires resolving its actual authority basis — attributable acceptance act, rightful holder, version, scope, applicability, conflict/supersession, review limits — **with unresolved conferral recorded separately from substantive support**; **using it as evidence, inference or a candidate** requires assessing its substance and basis and **no OMNI acceptance act at all.** *A successor must not have to guess which sentence applies to it.* FAI's worked `DL-18` case is the proof: content promotion **executed**, promotion mechanic **still flagged**, canonical location **transitional** — *a `LOCKED` header cannot tell a reader those three things.* **Do NOT rank sources on one authority ladder.**

> **The operator's question, answered rather than deflected — *"what if a heavy, adversarially-survived, unratified document is better than a ratified one?"* (FAI `§4.18.2`, verbatim).** **A `review-draft`, `frozen`, unratified or historical source may hold the strongest relevant reasoning and may change a proposed account immediately.** Its label is **not** grounds to dismiss it, and its quality is **not** grounds to self-promote it. **Where accepted state would change, that requires a proposed reopening and impact disposition — but thought and candidate formation wait for nothing.** **The reverse asymmetry is equally forbidden:** FAI `§E15.17.5` records external vendor documentation freely changing a candidate model while strong internal `analysis_nonbinding` work was treated as inert. **Authority classification governs what may BIND; it never decides whether a source may materially IMPROVE a candidate.** And depth is not immunity — FAI corrected attractive Reactor and Care claims after following their successors and forensic dispositions.

> **Preserve the resolution so the next agent reuses it instead of re-deriving it.** A material reliance resolution is recorded in `§13.2` with its **claim · intended use · exact references · limits · revalidation trigger.** **A successor checks currency and may reuse it WITH ATTRIBUTION** — it does not reconstruct unchanged authority history by default, **and it does not count another agent's reading as its own.** *Without this the failure simply moves: from "nobody checked the authority" to "every replacement spent its context rechecking the same authority history." Neither produces the operating model.*

**⑦ Semantic transfer, not document promotion — FAI R8 `§0.6`.** **Two movements, independent, and conflating them is the failure.** **(a) Scope placement:** is the finding domain-native · a cross-owner seam effect · a profile/applicability specialization · a proposed shared system rule · or evidence with no architectural transfer? **A proposed shared rule must show failure physics that survives removal of the domain nouns and recurs across materially unlike owners — not merely read well when generalized.** **(b) Architecture-subject specialization:** applying a distinction to architecture management *itself* is a **different subject** requiring its own object, owner, authority, records, clocks, commitments, execution vehicle, consequences and remedy. *Recommendation ≠ committed order and architecture-proposal ≠ accepted-architecture can share physics without the clinician's authority transferring to an architecture reviewer.* **Downward and cross-owner use is by versioned reference, applicability resolution, local admission and specialization — never a normative copy pasted into every contract.** FAI's pharmacy recovery is the witness: reading part of the source produced *"never auto-reroute"* and a universal resolver; **completing it narrowed the first** (authorized deterministic fallback existed) **and withdrew the second** (the controller was profile-specific).

**⑧ Limbs are not a composed law — FAI carrier `§4.18.3`.** That section **withdrew** the reassuring claim that nearly everything already had a home: several sources held **limbs** while **no source held the composed law**. **Four citations establishing four ingredients do NOT establish that anyone specified, accepted or implemented the relationship among them.** State separately: source support · semantic home · composition · governance · realization · **exact residual.** **"Already covered" is not a disposition — point to the operative instruction and its effect.** Conversely, before restoring older wording as *"lost intelligence,"* **read why it was withdrawn**: FAI's own silent-deletion recovery briefly restored material whose removal had been justified.

**Intake (`§4.2`).** Recovery is evidence reconstruction, not ratification, and establishes nothing about whether the recovered material is good. **Every recovered proposition takes exactly one disposition:** preserved · sharpened · narrowed · relocated · superseded · rejected · retained-as-evidence · **invent** · **experiment** · **open decision**. Every candidate declares its basis — *source-derived · operator-directed · implementation-evidenced · externally informed · experimentally supported · inferred · novel synthesis* — and the one prohibition is presenting an inference as source-derived or accepted. **No candidate requires an estate ancestor.**

**The choice-versus-fact rule, which settles who may decide what in `§12.7`.** Architecture contains both. *Whether OMNI serves disconnected operators* is a choice and the operator holds it; on a genuine choice within rightful authority **the operator's decision is the answer and needs no evidentiary defence.** *Whether a copy carries its source's authority* is not a choice and **nobody** holds it — not the operator, not a reviewer, not consensus. Treating a legitimate operator choice as needing external justification is as wrong as treating an operator assertion about reality as settling it.

**Consolidation (`§4.2`), and the structural obligation on this carrier.** *"A carrier that accumulates source → finding → correction → objection → another finding is an incident channel, not an architecture."* **That has been this carrier's shape.** R10 claimed to have moved withdrawal narration out of the operative sections and had not. **R11 shortened revision-specific retellings and corrected the named conflicting instructions. Historical explanatory notes remain, and that is intended — `§4.2` preserves discovery order and lineage rather than dissolving them.** **A keyword count is not proof of completed semantic consolidation**, and R11's report used one as its closing test. Lineage lives in `§16`; the operative text states the current position. Discovery order, decision lineage and supersession are **preserved, not dissolved** — a reader who can state the current law but cannot recover the reasoning will re-derive it. **A successful recovery makes the result smaller and clearer, not larger and better footnoted.**

**Operativity (`§4.3`), applying to anything this arc admits.** Six questions: **what kind of obligation · where it is realized · who owns the decision or enforcement · what observable condition constitutes failure · how applicability affects it · what evidence demonstrates satisfaction.** Three admissible states per question — answered, explicitly not-applicable with its reason, or a named acceptance blocker with an owner. **Unanswered is not among them**, and a law that compiles to nothing is rationale. *Mechanically operable is not machine-decided:* OMNI does not automate a judgment that belongs to its rightful holder; it automates whether the required judgment, authority, evidence, scope and proof exist.

**§13.1 Method law** *(arc-local, subordinate to `§13.0`)*. `1` Inheritance verification precedes assertion, not capture — capture provisionally, verify before asserting anything as new, settled or authoritative. `2` **A comparator claim is a novelty claim** and owes the same check. `3` **Before proposing a structural decomposition, consume the operative structural account of the most recent joint/cross-domain run — not its closeout.** `4` Source floor declared and discharged per output; delegated scans labelled as evidence, not an authored read. `5` One carrier. `6` No parallel lanes before G0 acceptance. `7` `METHOD-000` default; one current-state surface (`§1`). `8` **External sources captured with provenance and maturity before any conclusion depends on them** — `research_demonstration` · `clinical_investigation` · `authorized_bounded_use` · `established_operational_capability` · `speculative_future_assumption`. A statute or specification is authoritative about its own content without proving a deployed capability. `9` No absence claim without its coverage; no history claim from a current-state search; **no causal claim about a past omission without evidence for the cause.** **`9a` — An absence claim must be produced by a verified-working search.** Repeated `rg --glob '*.md' .` invocations in this session returned **silently empty** on terms that demonstrably exist (`delegated_authority_envelope`, `capability_envelope`, `ai_participation_policy`). **Several R5/R6 absence claims were therefore artifacts of a failing tool call, not findings about the estate** — a mechanical contributor to this arc's repeated under-inheritance that is independent of routing. **Cross-check every absence with a second, differently-shaped query before asserting it.** → `D0ECB-REV-007`. `10` **Expose the basis of a load-bearing claim (`§4`) — without forcing a box.** `11` **Stop framing each recovered passage as the biggest discovery yet.** `12` **Preserve the ambition and the contract together (`§2.1`); neither may be deleted in favour of the other.**

**§13.2 Live source-to-consequence control.** Per `§13.0` ①/⑤ each entry carries **path · section · status · controlling terminus**, because a path alone invites the label-as-disposition error this arc has already made.

**This is working state, not a retrospective receipt** *(FAI Output-4 `§E15.17.8`, adopted as behaviour)*. From the first activated unit it is **maintained as reads occur**, so that *what was inspected · exact sections · by whom · at what depth · source authority and relevant adjudication · native mechanism recovered · the ECB claim, decision or output affected · semantic consequence · what was explicitly NOT inspected · what remains next* is answerable **at any moment**, not only at a pass boundary. Five columns:

`Source · ref · exact sections · reader · depth` | `Authority, maturity, supersession, relevant adjudication` | `Native mechanism or finding` | `ECB claim / decision / output affected` | `Disposition · uninspected scope · next required action`

**Rules, inherited verbatim in substance:** **no row may terminate at "read," and no row owes an empty field** · **depth follows consequence** — a source with no material consequence earns a compact semantic receipt, one that merely confirms earns only the consequence, one that sharpens, contradicts, splits, relocates or exposes missing law earns full relational treatment · **a read has TWO triggers, and ECB had inherited only the first** *(the fuller FAI rule already carried both; see `§16`)* — **(a) claim verification:** inspect what supports or contradicts a claim the account retains; **(b) requirement discovery:** inspect material likely to expose something the account has **not** represented, prompted by the assignment, a native owner's boundary, prior unresolved work, an external mechanism, a changed assumption, or independent challenge. **An agent may NOT conclude that nothing material remains merely because every sentence it chose to write carries a citation.** Neither trigger is breadth-for-its-own-sake · **admitting a new source mid-unit is normal and resets nothing** · **no coverage percentage and no closed denominator may be published** — we do not possess the denominator, and a percentage asserts one. **The available completion claim has two halves and both must be stated: *no known material source gap behind a retained claim*, AND *this is the discovery work performed to look beyond the current account, with what it found*.** The first alone is the failure mode above. **No second table, ledger, registry, schema or tracker is created for this** — the five columns are the mechanism, and a parallel tracker becomes the competing copy `§13.5` already refuses.

**LAYER STATEMENT — required where a territory has been formulated more than once** *(added R36; operator-identified)*. **The recurring estate failure is not only that agents re-derive what exists — it is that when depth IS clawed back, nobody can tell WHICH layer came back:** the old superseded doctrine, the fresher reformulation, an invalidated conclusion, or a mix. **So a claim resting on a multiply-formulated territory must state, in one line: which formulation is current · which are superseded or demoted to evidence · which were invalidated · and what each contributed.** *(Verified instances rather than a worry: `care_obligation`/`care_commitment` spans several formulations whose current disposition is an **open realization choice** — `§7.4.2` — and the federation arc is a **split object** whose activated half lives in `DL-21`.)* **The estate already supplies the exemplar and it is not a new mechanism: `ordered_fulfillment_contract.md`'s binding *consolidation statement* names its controlling source, demotes the un-parked predecessor to *"evidence/provenance,"* asserts *"no staleness"* against the freshest framing, and lists what stays OPEN at ratification.** The Care forensic audit's `§F` INVALIDATED / `§G` proven split is the second exemplar, already routed. **No tracker, ledger or registry is created for this — the statement lives in the `§13.2a` row and the claim that depends on it.**

**Every retained load-bearing claim must be adequately supported, narrowed, or carry an explicit unresolved dependency with its acceptance effect. "Listed under remaining work" does not discharge it.** A bounded open-world search for omitted mechanisms and contrary evidence is permitted and expected: **an unknown source is not automatically irrelevant, an inaccessible source is not a passing result, and a missing peripheral read does not block unrelated work.**

**Decision-territory coverage — the map must reach past the sources whose vocabulary already resembles the hypotheses.**

| Decision territory | Necessary recovery coverage |
|---|---|
| whole operating arrangement (`D1`,`D2`) | joint care/business synthesis and its adjudication · Care and its **applicable forensic dispositions** · relevant system/surface and native lifecycle boundaries |
| authority, context, participation (`D1`,`D3`) | Trust/GCE lineage · capability **versus** delegation · Identity · Federation · RBAC · D7 · CNS · Agent Runtime · **paused Authority dependencies stated, not resolved here** |
| readiness and supporting business work (`D2`,`D5`) | scheduling · resources · workforce · settings/configuration · sourcing · inventory · fulfillment · commerce, including recorded ownership residuals |
| generated interventions and research (`D2`,`D3`) | trial/research · observation · material and identity lineage · consent-purpose · clinical memory · translational evidence |
| change, exposure, continuing responsibility (`D5`) | temporal · correction-impact · Care/Platform/Accountability · exposure · containment · transfer · obligation survival |
| comparative value and execution burden (`D6`) | `EVRUN` product/burden and fixture work · Task-D's scoped requirements · primary external evidence the actual comparisons need |
| prior routed learning | the existing AI-corpus routes, **with native anchors reopened when a transfer becomes load-bearing** |

**Resolve these through live routes and termini. This is a coverage obligation, not permission to invent ownership and not a requirement for an indiscriminate sweep.**

> **⚠ THIS MAP WAS NOT OBEYED IN UNIT 1, AND THE FAILURE IS RECORDED HERE BECAUSE THIS IS THE RULE THAT WAS BROKEN (R42, operator-identified).** Unit 1 read its assigned `EVRUN-000012` packet, then followed corrections into OFC, Care and the accepted forensic ledger — **and never derived its field from what its CLAIMS' SCOPE required.** The territories above name Trust/GCE, Federation, RBAC, CNS, Agent Runtime, scheduling, resources, settings, sourcing, inventory, commerce, trials/research, temporal, correction-impact, exposure and containment. **A seven-revision argument about ownership, projections, controllers, reopening, scarcity and economic influence was conducted while the financing, demand, scheduling, pharmacy, research, messaging and reservoir bodies that already develop those exact mechanisms sat unopened.** **That is the FAI `§E4c` failure quoted two paragraphs below, committed by the seat that quoted it: the population inherited the entry packet's blind spot, and the Care/commerce vocabulary decided what got noticed.** **The correction is not a wider sweep or a new framework — it is that a claim about ownership goes to the ownership bodies, a claim about scarcity goes to the scheduling and resource bodies, and a claim about economic influence goes to the financing body, before the claim is retained.** Corrections landed at `§7.4.2`, `§7.4.6`, `§7.4.7`, `§15.4a` and `§15.4b`; what was actually read versus reported is at `§13.2a`.

> **A WORKING SEARCH AND AN OFFICIAL ROUTE STILL DO NOT ESTABLISH THE RIGHT SOURCE POPULATION — FAI `§E4c`, the failure this arc is most likely to repeat.** FAI followed a named route, read the named source table **and** ran a bounded open-world step — and the population **inherited the route's blind spot**: it *"derived its source population from a route that never named it, and the exclusion manufactured a false completeness claim,"* with the defect arriving *"at the source-population step rather than the closeout step."* **The consequence was substantive: an outpatient-shaped authority account was being treated as cross-enterprise architecture**, and opening the omitted hospital, oncology and Insurance pressure work changed the boundaries. **The fix is not more keywords.** **Derive the field from what the CLAIM'S SCOPE requires:** a cross-setting claim needs the cross-setting pressure work · a multi-principal claim needs independent-principal cases · an economic-neutrality claim needs the actual incentive and burden work · and **the detailed native body must be followed wherever a compact contract does not carry the answer.** **Live catalog and read graph are necessary and insufficient: they help derive the population and are never allowed to declare their own omissions irrelevant.** **Review must challenge the SELECTION RULE and the omitted families, not only the support for sentences already written** (`§12.4` stop).

> **At the affected output boundary, three axes stay SEPARATELY answerable — FAI `§G1-CONTRACT.b`, installed there because *"a finding is not a control."*** **① acquisition** — what was actually obtained and read, by whom; **`UNAVAILABLE_AFTER_TESTED_ATTEMPT` is an honest acquisition result and NEVER an adequacy result.** **② materiality** — does this family bear on *this* conclusion; **`NOT_YET_ASSESSED` blocks acceptance, because an unassessed materiality is a reason to stop, never a reason to proceed**, and *"not currently cited"* is not a non-impact argument. **③ independent adequacy** — the reviewing seat's judgment that the evidence justifies the conclusion offered: adequate · **claim must be narrowed** · blocking. **Author acquisition and materiality (① and ②) stay distinct from reviewing-seat adequacy (③)** — FAI records the cost of conflating them as *"three passes went into the corpus and none into the diff,"* i.e. **misdirected effort**, which is what that sentence says and all it says. These live in the existing `§13.2` rows and `§15.4` — **no new matrix, enum or tracker.** **Prior accepted work is not retroactively annulled** by a later control: it is *not demonstrated against it*, and a material undispositioned dependency triggers **scoped revalidation**, not erasure.

**ECB's read-depth correspondence in `_07 §7.6`, stated so it is decidable rather than inferred.** ECB is **not** one of `_07`'s five named destinations. **This paragraph GOVERNS the RECONCILE floor; the ranges in the table below are the INITIAL BOUNDED PACKET, and where they are narrower the remainder is owed, not cancelled.** **ECB's RECONCILE units read at the C4-spine-author plus C5-pass depth** — which together require `_02 §15`, `_04`, `_07 §7.4`, `_07 §7.9`, `_00 §3`, `_02 §§5`–`6`, `_05` **including its operator-configuration, sourcing, payment, firewall and surfaces material** *(the `_07` C5 consumption contract — these cannot be satisfied by the table's narrower `§5.0`–`§5.6.1` range)*, and `_06` R2/R6. **That is a materially larger set than `§12.9.3`'s entry packet, and the difference is the packet's remaining reach, not an optional extra.**

No fourth scheme.)*

**Not deferred, and not consumed: materiality UNASSESSED.** The **six AI-corpus registries** and the **native system/surface and lifecycle-boundary material** named in the territory map sit here. **`§13.2`'s own rule governs: an unassessed materiality is a reason to stop, never a reason to proceed** — so no `§7.4` claim may rest on them, and none does.

**The operating decision this accounting forces — and at R45 it is executed in the account, not declared beside it.** R44 stated the narrowing in this table while the operative text still carried the claims it was narrowing; **a limitation recorded next to a claim does not narrow the claim.** What changed in `§7.4` at R45: **three `§7.4.8` items were REMOVED** rather than relabelled (the vendor-count scale prediction, the highest-leverage ranking, the incumbent comparison) · **`PD-8` was rewritten from *"no conclusion is possible"* to a supported NOT-DISCHARGED account** with its three specific blockers · **the firewall paragraph now carries the D6 scope gap and `§G2`'s `OC-AUTHZ` defect** instead of a maturity dispute · and **four positions the carrier had been stating as its own are re-attributed to the temporal charter** (`§7.4.8` ①). **The standing rule: cohort claims stand at cohort scope; a claim reaching past it is either supported by a read recorded in this workbench, admitted as an alternative carrying its own conditions, explicitly cohort-scoped, or removed.**

**The discovery half is the part an author can silently skip, and `§13.2` already says so:** the obligation is not only to support what was written but to look for what the account has **not** represented — *"an agent may NOT conclude that nothing material remains merely because every sentence it chose to write carries a citation."* **The honest position on both halves (R44): bounded discovery DID occur inside and around the original cohort** — `_01`, `_03`, `_00 §3.5` and the fixture bodies each surfaced material the account had omitted — **but neither half is discharged: support for the WIDER claims is incomplete, and no adequate selection of the wider field has been demonstrated.** *Replacing "insufficient discovery" with "no discovery" would be its own overcorrection.*

**What this unit CLAIMED, in the two halves `§13.2` requires — half (a) now withdrawn.** **(a)** No known material source gap stands behind a claim retained in `§7.4` — every retained claim carries its section-level attribution, and the three claims that turned out to be sited elsewhere than expected are named as `INT-1`/`INT-2`/`INT-3` rather than left implicit. **(b)** The discovery work performed to look beyond the account already written was: reading `_01` and `_03` in full when the entry packet named only `_07`/`_02 §15`/`_04`; reading `_02 §14` when `§15`'s omissions made it answer-bearing; reading `_00 §3.5` and `§0.6` when the maturity question required the scope ruling; and running the two-shape absence check. **It found four things the account had not represented** — `_00 §3.5`'s pre-figuring of ECB's own scope discipline, `_07 §7.5`'s disposition normalization, `_01`'s dropped fourth falsifier, and `_03`'s unretired `C6` falsifier. **None was reachable from the entry packet's vocabulary**, which is `§13.2`'s point.

Verified: the read graph **does** reference `EVRUN-000012` (**four occurrences on three lines, via routes `9i`, `9k` and `9m`** — R15 reported three via two routes; the conclusion that a retrieval path existed is unaffected and slightly strengthened). **Correcting the stale attribution in this carrier was NOT itself authorization to write route `#9w` or the catalog row; **the operator authorized both at R31 and they are now landed on this branch** (`§14`, `§15.4`); this merely prevents an incorrect route list from landing later**, and the run carries an index plus explicit deep-consumption pointers. **So there was a retrieval path.** The accurate finding is narrower: **there is no direct task-entry route, the catalog run-row points at `_00`, and `_07` warns against being substituted for `_02 §15` — a discoverability weakness worth fixing.** It cannot explain missing a C3.7 passage in a source already consulted, or a Palantir passage in an already-routed source. **Those were reading failures.** A route is a useful improvement and **is not this arc's achievement.**

**§13.4 Preservation homes — part of acceptance, not a closeout chore.** **Thesis/spine:** `§2.1`'s ambition **and** contract, with mechanism, limits and evidence maturity. **Architecture capture / reference model:** the execution, authority, semantic-dependency, temporal, uncertainty and control relationships. **Domain / seam / profile contracts:** applicable conditions, responsible owners, validity and refresh rules, **reservation and commitment semantics**, response requirements, evidence contracts. **Build and evaluation:** fixtures, fault injection, observability, burden measurement, external validation. **Different homes for different responsibilities — not four maintained copies of the same prose.** Exact paths and accepted successors resolve before any write; `/architecture` installation and FAI remain separately controlled. **This carrier is the review home until a destination accepts a finding — not a licence to mark everything C5-later.**

**§13.5 One carrier. A review is not a second carrier.** The preservation diagnosis is accepted — ten passes produced review notes, and six good reviews do not become one usable memory. **But the remedy offered was a second maintained document** with its own version number, section structure, source register, maintenance rule (*"when a material claim changes, update its operative wording…"*) and consumption contract. **That is a competing carrier, and it is rejected on three grounds:** the collaboration model gives the repository writing seat to one party and review to the other — a reviewer maintaining a parallel operative account inverts it · `D0CKPT-GRD-003` forbids duplicated live state, and two documents that both claim to hold the current working account **will drift and become indistinguishable from real divergence at boot** — the exact failure this estate has paid for repeatedly · `D0TIER0-GRD-002` requires any governed stream artifact to carry its operating contract, review gate and maintenance owner **in the same pass as its creation**, which an external file cannot satisfy. **The content is adopted; the vessel is not.** Review findings land **in this carrier**, in its own sections, under one version history — and where a review's own reasoning is worth preserving verbatim, it is captured as **evidence with its authorship intact**, never as a second current-state surface. **The preservation problem was never that we lacked a second document. It was that the one document contained contradictory instructions** — which `§15.1` and the four repairs above fix.

## §15 — Gate-0 stop receipt

### §15.0 — Plan acceptance and G1 activation are two separate operator acts

**Accepting this plan does not start the investigation.** No sentence in this carrier may combine them.

```
[x] PLAN ACCEPTED       — DONE. Design accepted R24 (2026-09-13); corrected
                          execution method accepted R28 (2026-09-14) after
                          Knox PASS. Both heads/blobs recorded in §1, which
                          owns this record. They are SEPARATE facts: R24
                          does not contain the R25–R28 method amendments
[~] G1 PASS 1 — NOT a single act, and this box is NOT a blanket switch.
                          Nick activates ONE bounded unit at a time.
                          RECONCILE unit 1: ACTIVATED 2026-09-14, RETURNED
                          at review. No later unit activated. §1 OWNS this
                          state — read it there, not here
```

**Box 1 — plan accepted at R24 by Nick, 2026-09-13. Box 2 — deliberately NOT a checkbox any more: "G1 activated" is not a state this carrier can hold truthfully, because activation is per-unit.** The current position, owned by `§1` and restated here only as a pointer: **G0 design and method acceptance stand at their respective baselines; RECONCILE unit 1 was separately activated and has returned for review; no later unit is activated.** *(Historical: through R32 this box read unchecked with "G1 NOT activated," which was true then and became false at unit-1 activation. Corrected R35 after review found `§15.0`/`§15.2` contradicting `§1`.)*

**How acceptance is recorded, because the previous mechanism was unusable.** R12–R15 bound acceptance to *"head and primary blob in the return receipt"* — a chat artifact. **Agent Work Protocol `§2.1` (L168) forbids exactly that: *"no agent may be required to recover a base SHA from a chat transcript,"*** and requires a base to be **bound after acceptance, never asserted inside the content commit it describes**. Checking a box here is itself a write, so the accepted bytes and the bytes recording acceptance can never be one object.

> **On acceptance, the authorized writer lands a STATE-ONLY receipt commit** recording the accepted content head and primary blob. **That commit is the acceptance record, and its owning location is `§1`'s accepted-baseline row — this section points there rather than holding a second copy.** The commit **records** an acceptance decision; it does not create one. **Until that commit exists the accepted baseline reads NONE.** *(Historical: that was the state through R24. It no longer is — acceptance was recorded 2026-09-13 and `§1` carries the accepted head and blob.)*

Each subsequent pass is activated the same way. The author returns **carrier changes · source-coverage receipt · findings · unresolved conditions** at each review stop (`§12.4`) and waits for release. **Where interacting findings or uncertain source authority, output ownership or promotion status accumulate, the author stops at a synthesis checkpoint earlier than the named stop** (`§13.0` ③). **No self-issued architecture acceptance. No whole-gate completion claim.**

**Produced by the current revision named at `§1`:** *(restated R35 — this sentence described plan-repair revisions and became false at R33)* **the return of RECONCILE unit 1 and its bounded corrections. Still: no architecture. No name minted. No scenario library. No promotion. No activation of any later unit.**

> **An illustrative pressure case, supplied by the review seat.** *Illustrative only — not a claim the estate lacks such a case, not scored, and not a `§11.6` population; `§11.6` stays empty until TRACE preparation (`§12.9.6`).*
>
> *A practice holds an established follow-up responsibility. Plan `P1` depends on an external service and a request has been sent, but **acceptance has not been observed**. An authorized decision then **supersedes `P1` with `P2`**. Later, the external party reports that it **accepted the original `P1`**.*
>
> **What must remain simultaneously distinguishable:** the late report may be **accurate about the original request** without authorizing performance under the current plan · the external party **may never have received the change** · the practice's follow-up responsibility **does not disappear because the counterparty was silent** · **a cancellation request is not proof of cancellation** · and **an accurate projection of all of that is still not the actor that resolves it.** The case demands an answer to **who responds now, under which authority, to which version, on what evidence, and what responsibility remains if the counterparty never answers again.**
>
> **Supersession is not proof of external cancellation or uptake.** Superseding an OMNI-side plan does not, by itself, establish that a related external request was amended or cancelled, that the counterparty received and applied the change, or that execution stopped. **A participant may retain or act on an older request without that request remaining authorized** — *present* and *authorized* are different, and the case must not answer with either. The case tests whether the inherited mechanisms distinguish **the applicable authority and version · communication and execution evidence · and continuing responsibilities** across that difference. **It establishes neither an estate-wide gap nor a required new protocol** — `_05 §5.5`/`§5.6` already carry supersession with impact assessment and explicit external acceptance or rejection, so the open question is **adequacy of the composition**, not a newly discovered absence. **Delay or non-receipt is a condition to examine in the particular arrangement, not a property of a communication mode; the ladder specifies attribution, explicit custody and preservation of unknown downstream state, and states no latency bound either way.** → **`Q-PROTO`** (what silence, timeout and partial fulfilment mean between parties) · **`Q-DEP`** (what a `supersedes` relation actually causes, including a notification duty) · `PD-8` · `D1`, `D3`, `D5`. **`H-CONC` is unchanged: its falsifier is two actors relying on one resource, not two parties holding different versions. No new hypothesis is added.**
>
> **Bounded contribution, DERIVED from `§13.2a`'s source-field accounting rather than asserted before it (R44).** **Question:** what conditions must hold for a named actor to act now and to continue acting, and how do the estate's existing realizations of that question combine, differ and fall short? **Output:** how the relevant mechanisms combine, where they differ, and what remains unresolved — **not** a solution to OMNI's composition and **not** an inventory of names.
>
> **What the accounting fixes about this unit's shape, with the three states kept apart (corrected R45 — R44 wrote *"unread by any ECB seat"* across a family the workbench itself records a reviewing seat as having sampled).** The distinction that matters is **this author's consumption · attributed reviewing-seat consumption · adequacy for a particular conclusion**, and collapsing them sends a successor back through work while still missing the real dependency. **Current state of the authority/context/participation family:** **CNS** is parent-verified in full by the accepted Care forensic audit (`§D`) — attributed, not ECB's read · **GCE, Federation, Platform, Accountability, Agent Runtime** were **sampled by the reviewing seat** and are recorded as such · **Platform's ownership-correction table and Accountability's coordinator/ledger and `response_obligation` provisions are now read by THIS seat** (`§13.2a`, R45) · **RBAC · Identity · `D7` · `D3` · Intake · Messaging remain unread BY THIS SEAT — but they ARE parent-read in the accepted Care Lane-1 checkpoints (`L1-CP1`/`CP2`, named sections plus shipped-code evidence), which is the third state: **attributed estate consumption, neither this author's read nor an absence.** *(R45 asserted the opposite from a superseded `§E` list; withdrawn R46.)*** **Corrected at R46, because R45's version **Four execution risks recorded rather than converted into new gates** *(no tracker, headcount, quota, campaign or object is added for them)*: **recovery displacing construction** — the first unit owes the inherited operating account with its disagreements and proof debt, and an impeccable source receipt must not purchase acceptance of an underdeveloped explanation · **prohibitions displacing useful behaviour** — *a system that accurately explains why it cannot act is not the operating substrate this is for*; enabling behaviour must survive consolidation · **a hard responsibility mistaken for a defensible business** — do not assume counterparties participate or that difficulty confers value, **and equally do not let "a competitor may win" become a default retreat: `D1`–`D4` keep a larger OMNI responsibility reachable on evidence** · **continuity rules mistaken for demonstrated continuity** — the next evidence comes from real bounded work, not another charter soundcheck.

**One lesson explicitly refused.** Recovering `_01`, `_03` and `_00` does **not** mean the operating model is already solved. It means more prior reasoning must now inform — and remain challengeable within — the investigation.
