## §16 — Amendment log

> **Registration status, stated once so it is not rediscovered — UPDATED AT R33:** both ECB documents are **catalogued, routed and ON `main`** — `9w` takes ECB execution work to this carrier, `9x` takes later arc-design work to the rationale record. **PR #20 was merged in `c54f53dc`; unit 1 verified both catalog rows and both routes present on the default branch. The registration debt that stood open from R1 to R30 is fully discharged, including publication.** *(Historical: R31 and R32 correctly stated the change was branch-only at the time they were written. That is no longer the state, and the R31/R32 entries below are lineage — read them as such.)*

> **Design rationale and method lineage:** `.cursor/plans/v4_ECB_G0_design_rationale_and_method_lineage_2026-09-14.md` — **why this arc chose what it chose, traced to source, plus what should not be copied forward.** `rationale_nonbinding`; **owns nothing and originates no instruction.** Filed beside this carrier under the `v4_` arc convention rather than in `docs/architecture/`, which holds estate-level architecture records, **not arc-local process reasoning.** Catalogued and routed at `9x` alongside this carrier's `9w`, in one change — see its `§6`.



**R1** `60619c4f` initial · **R2** `3b9db0c2` nine Knox amendments; *"2035 comes free"* withdrawn · **R3** `88e65c07` maturity ladder dissolved; governance made a constraint plane · **R4** `93dab6aa` scope de-clinicalized from Care `§1a`; naming deferred per `GRD-026`; Palantir withdrawn · **R5** `0bbfa91c` `EVRUN-000012 §15` recovered and installed as inherited structure; lattice demoted; speed thesis introduced.

**R47** — **the account consolidated onto a RATIFIED spine that no ECB seat had opened, plus four corrections where a related mechanism was described as the missing one.**

**The finding that should have come first and came forty-six revisions late.** `§7`'s inheritance list cites **GCE** as *"(`D0THES-DEC-036`, **ratified**)"* — one clause, a decision ID, and **no seat ever opened the capstone.** It is at `doctrine/omni_enterprise_posture_2026-06-03.md`, it is **`ratified` 2026-06-03 · `governance_binding`**, and it is **the highest-authority source any ECB seat has read.** It states the spine: `actor / represented-principal → capability contract → Identity → Federation boundary → RBAC capability → delegated authority → context packet → consent/grant → CNS orchestration → owning-domain commit → audit/proof → returned artifact/status` — classified **evidence | observation | proposed-meaning | `externally_committed_truth`** *(committed in the source system, NOT OMNI-committed)* ***"BEFORE it counts."*** And it answers, at ratified authority, the question this arc has been circling: ***"Two faces, one spine"* — internal and external, *"the governance spine is IDENTICAL,"* with external adding only trust-transfer and metadata-custody risk.** With `GRD-034` (*"NOT measured by number of integrations; measured by whether every exchange preserves identity, authority, consent, context, ownership, commit, and proof"*) and `GRD-033` (*"rail-agnostic, protocol-agile, vendor-replaceable, semantics-stable… replaceable adapters"*), **the connector layer is deliberately the commodity and the six owned things are the product.**

**`§7.4.9` is rewritten from a synthesis checkpoint into the consolidated operating account the review asked for** — what the arrangement does · which authority decides what · what may remain concurrently open · which candidate addresses which part · **eight specific missing relationships** · portability · simulation · and the checkpoint. **A catalogue of what remains unread is not an operating account**, and `§12.9.6` makes `§7.4` the home of the current one.

**Four corrections, all one class: a related mechanism described as the missing mechanism.**

| # | withdrawn | corrected |
|---|---|---|
| **1** | RBAC's rechecks at emission and execution as *"an inherited throughout-execution recheck for `H-DUR`"* | **Two checkpoints — or many — are not continuing control.** An act can pass at queue time and again at start while a relied-upon condition changes mid-run, and **neither check establishes who detects that or what follows.** `H-DUR` now separates **① point-of-action authorization** (RBAC at emission/execution; Agent Runtime at *every* consequential boundary, *"NOT once at run start"*) · **② continued-use invalidation reaching a LIVE run** — Agent Runtime invalidates continued-use authorization, future admissibility and caches, the run may **pause/terminate/narrow/reassemble**, the transition is recorded, and **the receipt of what the run saw stays immutable** — the closest inherited relative · **③ supervision of a continuously unfolding act, NOT established anywhere read** |
| **2** | `SC-L1-09` as *"a concrete instance of `C6`'s failure"* | **A concrete accepted derivation/ownership gap, which is not the registered falsifier.** `C6` is *a state transitions correctly while nobody performs the required response*; an unspecified derived view might instead produce incorrect membership, inconsistent visibility or ambiguous assignment, **and an explicitly assigned duty could be performed without that view at all.** A design trace *could* demonstrate `C6` — the bar is not runtime evidence — but it must name the transition, the remaining responsibility, its dependence on the missing derivation, and the absent response path. **`SC-L1-09` does none of those.** *The rule this keeps breaking: a nearby defect is not necessarily the registered falsifier* |
| **3** | *"thesis `§C` never authored"* as the decisive GCE account | **An unauthored thesis section does not make a separately RATIFIED capstone nonexistent.** Four layers stay apart: the **ratified capstone** (posture and governing distinctions) · the **July Care audit** (contract-expression and enforcement findings in its scope) · **`C4.6`**, Knox-PASSED 2026-07-31, a developed specialization that *explicitly consumes* the capstone · the **Agent Runtime bridge** (bounded context/authority/invalidation, wider runtime open). **And `C4.6` supplies the precise external-truth rule, sharper than repeating *"external systems are never truth owners"*: an external system MAY be authoritative for facts committed in its own sovereign scope while owning none of OMNI's canonical state** |
| **4** | *"the missing part is the policy instance"* for front-desk access | **The accepted audit distinguishes four things: policy/granularity questions · drafted enforcement absent from shipped code · partially implemented behaviour (Messaging's send-policy gate, with named steps implemented and named steps deferred) · and behaviour *"not evidenced in the paths read."*** Filling in a permission table would not finish enforcement, and the code findings stay attributed to that bounded July inspection rather than becoming a current-build census |

**Two internal contradictions the accounting table was carrying, fixed.** It still recorded `D6 §8`'s body as unread and *"prevents any `PD-8` conclusion"* **while later passages described those reads and stated that conclusion.** *Labelling a table a current accounting surface does not make its rows agree with the account.* `PD-8` is now **NOT DISCHARGED with named blockers** — which is a conclusion, correctly bounded — and the `D6` row records the R45 read with the scope it supports.

**Simulation admitted at its actual status, on the operator's prompting, in existing homes.** **Two jobs that must not collapse:** testing the arrangement before exposing real participants *(an uncalibrated model can reveal a counterexample; it establishes no probability, safety property or economics)* and **informing an operating decision** *(assumptions, scope and evidence quality stay distinguishable from observed state and accepted commitments)*. **Neither creates an OMNI Simulation domain.** Inherited: `EVSRC-2026-000242`'s Simulation Lab is **watch-stage, not accepted**, and refuses synthetic behaviour as consent, clinical truth or outcome proof; FAI `§E15.4.1` supplies **prospective assessment · simulated consequence · observed consequence.** **One inherited inference NARROWED rather than adopted:** that an execution producing no commitment-frontier event *"did not touch the world."* **Absence of that record is not proof of absence of effect unless effect mediation and recording coverage are established** — otherwise an unrecorded send or an out-of-band action becomes *"simulation"* by omission, and the same passage already concedes a shadow path may expose real data. *(A review conclusion about a frozen formulation. FAI is not reopened; no simulator source is added to the floor.)*

**Portability stated from the ratified posture rather than re-derived.** Semantics-stable with replaceable rails means **portability cannot degrade by participant type — the spine does not branch on what the counterparty is, only on trust-transfer and metadata-custody risk.** `C4.6` makes it concrete: **origin context is ORTHOGONAL** (medspa · asynchronous · ambulatory · emergency · inpatient · discharge) and **a medication list is *not itself* an executable prescription.** **Receiving material confers no authority, renews no permission and establishes no acceptance**, and Runtime supplies the counterpart: preserve what a run saw while invalidating its permission to keep using it.

**And the arc's crudest question, answered where the estate answers it and left open where it does not.** **`P35` — *"External Capability / Signal-Command Boundary"* — was deliberately renamed from *"Device-Link / Telemetry-Ownership"* as *"too device-flavored"* so that it spans *"ANY external system… (watch, vent, HR monitor, **Tesla surgical robot**, lab analyzer, IV pump, phone/PBX, cafeteria system, camera…)."*** **So an async questionnaire and a robot cutting tissue share the boundary object and the governance spine — and that is where the comparison STARTS.** **`§6.3`'s caveat governs the rest and is not superseded by shared scope: *"an adequate asynchronous review arrangement is not automatically an adequate real-time supervisory arrangement."*** **The one dimension on which they genuinely diverge is command-authority mode — which has NO owner and no positive enforcement carrier at contract depth (`REV-188` open).** That is `D4`'s subject and it is now named rather than gestured at.

**R43's own overcorrection, withdrawn.** R43 asserted that Insurance's `A-Q12` **is** `PD-8` and therefore the alternative was *"circular."* **They overlap and are not identical:** `A-Q12` asks who owns a derived assessment's computation, versioning, invalidation and issuance; `PD-8` asks whether the distributed mechanisms actually discharge an unresolved responsibility. **They come apart — a financing assessment can have an assigned maintainer, correct versioning and timely recomputation while the responsible participant still fails to act on an unresolved external request.** And **an alternative with unresolved details is not circular or inadmissible; it becomes unsound only if those details are assumed resolved in order to claim the requirement discharged.** *Stronger self-criticism is not stronger reasoning.* **Also guarded:** the documentation-versus-implementation distinction holds **regardless of ratification**, and does **not** license *the container is a draft, therefore every invariant in it is provisional* — authority and implementation evidence stay separate.

**Added at R38 — one illustrative case from the review seat and one finding this seat drew from it.** The case: a follow-up responsibility with `P1` sent, **acceptance unobserved**, an authorized decision **superseding `P1` with `P2`**, and then the external party reporting it **accepted `P1`.** Recorded at `§15.4b` **at its author's stated status — illustrative, not a claim the estate lacks such a case, not scored, and not a `§11.6` population.** **What it exposes: SUPERSESSION IS NOT A BROADCAST.** OMNI can version its own instruction, but a superseded instruction may stay live at an independent participant that never received the change — and may then be performed. The inherited cascade governs OMNI-side reopening, **not counterparty-side acceptance of a superseded version** — so at the external boundary `supersedes` must answer **superseded for whom, from when, and with what obligation to reach the participant**, with Modes 3–4 making propagation delay unbounded. **And it is a second face of `H-CONC`: contention not over a scarce thing but over WHICH INSTRUCTION IS CURRENT, between two authorities with different states and no shared clock.**

**Where this seat declines the reviewer's own framing, on the same grounds the reviewer used against R36.** The review states that OFC's *"freshest framing"* claim cannot bind over the July estate — **correct** — and then treats July `§5`'s four options as the current position. **But the review itself reports that the capture's forensic consolidation and correction was NEVER PERFORMED**, and this seat verified that independently: the handoff makes consolidation the *next authorized transaction*, capture FROZEN, corrections routed through a proposed Care-correction ledger before any capture edit. **So `§5` is a July formulation inside an UNCORRECTED capture, and whether the accepted Lanes touched it is unverified by either seat** — the review consulted the ledger *"deeply but not exhaustively."* **The finding is almost certainly right and `§5` is a strong, self-aware, `[OPEN]`-marked passage. Its STATUS is what must not be upgraded by being recovered.** *Applying the reviewer's rule to the reviewer's source is not a counter-attack; it is the rule working.*

**And the reframed question needs one clause it does not have, or the next unit re-runs Care's arc instead of ECB's.** The reviewer's version — *which responsibilities and accepted commitments must remain distinguishable, how are they represented and related, and what preserves them when decisions, orders, participants or authority change* — is better than R36's and **every clause is satisfiable inside OMNI.** ECB's subject is the **external** boundary, so the operative form adds: **…and which of those distinctions must survive a participant OMNI does not control, who never adopted OMNI's representation, and who may refuse, disappear, or withhold.** Without that clause this is a Care question.

| **1** | *"no owner by construction"* is wrong — it turns an unresolved composition question into alleged structural impossibility | **ADOPTED IN FULL, and the defect is worse than reported.** A controller's bounded remit does not prevent it owning a response originating elsewhere; one lifecycle closing releases nothing automatically; coexisting responsibilities need no universal owner. **`_07 L6`'s two-word residual `controller boundaries` does not establish the stronger claim either — also withdrawn.** **Two aggravations this seat found, not the reviewer:** R35's headline example (**paid-but-undelivered**) is `_00 §3.3` `Q7`'s **best-composed meaning** — composed from OFC + D6 + Federation + Accountability + the 252 ladder — **so this seat chose the estate's strongest composition to illustrate a structural absence, having read `Q7` during unit 1**; and the *"something must own the seam"* instinct would have breached **OFC `§5`'s binding anti-junk-drawer discriminator**, *"`care_obligation` is NOT a universal task table."* **The corrected statement is symmetric: the account assigns profile-specific controllers AND carries explicit obligation/ownership/assignment/escalation mechanisms; that does not establish they compose at every seam — and existing owner fields do not prove the contract works.** `C6` stays live |

Replaced with *"do not make dependable operation conditional on owning the counterparty's interface,"* plus the reviewer's sharper point that **the rung number decides nothing** — what matters is what evidence arrives, its attributability, what it establishes, freshness for this use, whether responsibility was **accepted**, and which actions it can support. **`PD-2` stays a measurable pilot obligation, and the slide from *"value does not require them to join"* to *"every capability works without their cooperation"* is explicitly guarded** |
| **3** | scarcity and substitutability are different problems | **ADOPTED — the sharpest analytical correction of the round.** A **fungible** item can have **one acceptable unit left**, where re-selection resolves nothing without an allocator; a **non-fungible** resource may have acceptable alternatives (another room, time, qualified person). **So fungibility neither implies nor denies that re-selection suffices.** Substitution decides which alternatives are acceptable; **allocation** decides which claimant may rely on which capacity. The live set is plural: existing **booking authority** · serialized acceptance · queue · reservation · negotiated commitment. **Extended by this seat: OMNI may need to REFERENCE or COORDINATE an existing authoritative allocator rather than BECOME one — the option most consistent with Layer 3's no-central-table law, since an existing scheduler is domain-owned truth reached by a typed relation.** `H-CONC` becomes a **bounded coverage debt**, and *"re-selection is the strongest simpler alternative"* is corrected to *an* inherited comparator that `H-SPEED` still requires to be **earned** |
| **4** | keep the cascade; do not turn four examples into the universal rule | **ADOPTED.** The run's own **`H3`** holds that equivalence does not bypass patient preference, consent, jurisdiction, pathway policy, availability or risk revalidation. **The deadline counterexample is decisive and new:** an authorization conditioned on performance before a declared deadline is not preserved by a product-equivalent substitute arriving after it — **equivalence of the thing is not equivalence of the governing basis.** Replaced with: *preserve commitments whose **governing basis** remains adequate · reassess decisions whose **material basis** changed · keep unresolved consequences visible and assigned without reopening unrelated work.* **And *"wrong kind of document"* is withdrawn** — `analysis_nonbinding` is a legitimate home for an unpromoted candidate and **scale is not a promotion argument**; the accurate statement is *not yet carried into the operative downstream contracts and realization needed to enforce it.* No contract write authorized |

Three questions separated: **ownership · reconstruction · what causes the next responsible action.** An immutable log answers only the second and *"can preserve an inaccurate assertion perfectly."* **And `read-only projection` ≠ `view over destructively overwritten records`, so `REV-090` may be ORTHOGONAL rather than a fourth position.** The surviving question is reframed as inherited content of `REQUIRES_EXTERNAL_ACCEPTANCE` — *what a counterparty must be able to verify in order to accept* — not a forecast |

**R32** — **the recovery workbench named exactly, and a wrong answer removed.**

**The operator's "integrator" question was about the source-recovery method, not a seat, and R31 answered the wrong one.** **R31's integrator-vacancy paragraph is removed from both documents** — it explained a seat nobody asked about and partly restated a rule already in `§12.9.7a`, which states outright that **concurrency alone creates no parent package, common base or integrator.** No capability is lost; the coordination controls, the durable-contribution declaration, AWP `§2.1`, and the writer/snapshot/transfer rules all stand untouched.

**The method actually being asked about is FAI's `§E15.17.8` live source-recovery control — and it is already inherited here, verbatim in substance, at `§13.2`.** Verified in the native source rather than asserted: the carrier carries *"no row may terminate at 'read', and no row owes an empty field"* and **depth follows consequence** — a confirming source earns a compact consequence record; one that **sharpens, contradicts, splits, relocates or exposes missing law** earns full relational treatment. **That asymmetry is the efficiency mechanism**, and it is the thing that would be lost if a future agent rebuilt bookkeeping from scratch. The rationale record now names `§E15.17.8` exactly and preserves the distinctive mechanics — five columns maintained *during* reading, the two rules, detail-follows-consequence, linked synthesis checkpoints, and read-completion versus reconciliation-completion — **pointing at `§13.2`/`§7.4`/`§15.4` instead of duplicating the procedure.** **It is a written working procedure, not an autonomous estate-survey tool, and no claim of measured efficiency is made.**

**Lanes are answered in the rationale record's existing continuity row rather than as a new concept:** the capability is retained without copying any prior arc's staffing, because an additional durable contribution must declare question, owner, input, output, writable boundary, dependency and disposition path **before activation**, and **coordination is earned by a real dependency or collision — sequenced or simultaneous — not by concurrency.**

**Also corrected:** the rationale record's passport still claimed registration was *proposed and owed* while its `§6` said the rows and routes had landed. **Passport now matches the diff** — registered on this branch, `main` publication separate. **And the withdrawn "six unchanged, six fixed" tally was repeated in a return after being retired; it is not repeated again. The individual `N1`–`N12` dispositions are the record, and counting twelve rows verifies nothing about their content.**

**R31** — **both ECB documents are registered. The discoverability debt that stood open from R1 to R30 is discharged on this branch.**

**What the debt actually was, since it was never plainly stated:** the catalog lists what exists and how to read its status; the read graph tells an agent *"for this kind of assignment, read these."* **Neither moves a file, promotes anything to doctrine, accepts an architectural conclusion, or starts an arc.** Registration was deferred for twenty-nine revisions because writes to those shared indexes needed an operator mandate this seat did not hold — **and then the deferral itself became the risk: two documents that nobody could find from a normal entry point.** The operator's direction closed it. **Two catalog rows landed; route `9w` takes ECB execution work to this carrier, route `9x` takes later arc-design or method-recovery work to the rationale record as conditional precedent.** Registering the companion while the parent stayed unregistered would have been incoherent, and so was leaving both — **they landed together.**

**Publication stated honestly:** these changes are on `cursor/ecb-g0-reconnaissance-6a09` in PR #20 and are **NOT on `main`. A branch-only change is not default-branch discoverability.** The remaining act is the merge decision, which is the operator's — **that is one identified action, not an indefinite "authorization owed."**

**The rationale record was also repaired** *(review found it real but compressed in the wrong places)*. **Deleted:** the directory-placement argument, which was self-justification rather than reusable intelligence. **Added in its place, three mechanisms it had flattened** — cumulative source-to-consequence recovery with synthesis checkpoints and replacement from preserved partial state · one-writer discipline with explicit transfer and head-versus-content identity · consolidation into Outputs A–D with the authorability test. **Corrected:** *"access overlaps; responsibility does not"* was too absolute — primary responsibilities differ and **each seat substantiates its own claims**; the catalog and read graph are **starting indexes used and challenged, not disregarded**; and external evidence and original reasoning are explicitly retained as legitimate candidate inputs. **Unsupported counts of agent contexts and failure instances removed; the documented failure mechanisms kept.**

**R30** — **one pointer added at `§16` to the design-rationale record. Nothing operative changed.**

**The operator's ask was simple and had been made repeatedly: write down, somewhere findable, what this arc chose, why, and from which sources — so the next designer does not re-derive it and the next executor is not handed rules with no visible origin.** That record is now at `.cursor/plans/v4_ECB_G0_design_rationale_and_method_lineage_2026-09-14.md`, `rationale_nonbinding`, owning nothing.

**Two review instructions were declined, with reasons recorded in that file's `§1` and `§6` rather than silently dropped.** The review seat proposed filing it in `docs/architecture/` as an evolution-narrative volume. **The series there is real** — nine volumes — **but that directory holds fifteen curated estate-level architecture records, while `.cursor/plans/` holds 194 files and is where every arc lives, including all of FAI and this carrier. An arc-design record is an arc artifact.** *A directory being tidy is not a reason to put things in it.* The review seat also directed writing a corpus-catalog row and a Major-Arc Intake precedent pointer; **declined at this moment, not on the merits — those are protected shared surfaces, this carrier's own catalog row and route `#9w` have stood OWED and unauthorized across twenty-nine revisions, and registering both under one authorization is the coherent act.** The rationale file states plainly that it is reachable by path and by this pointer, and **not yet discoverable through catalog or read graph.**

Knox returned **`PASS`** on the R28 corrections with **no further substantive Gate-0 amendment requested** (`§15.7` `RV-11`), and the operator closed the planning loop on 2026-09-14. **Two acceptances are now recorded as SEPARATE facts in `§1`, deliberately** — the **investigation design** at R24 and the **corrected execution method** at R28 — because R24's bytes do not contain the R25–R28 amendments and **a successor must not read them as if they did.** That is `§12.9.7a`'s object→method rule applied to this carrier's own state, which is the first place it should have been applied.

**Unchanged by this closure:** **G1 is not activated** and no writer is active — activation is a separate operator act naming the first writer, authorizing **one bounded unit**, not the phase, the gate or a fleet. Catalog registration, route `#9w` and evidence-lane filing under `GRD-037` **remain owed at their recorded status**; this closure discharges none of them. And the standing that matters most for whoever arrives next: **Gate-0 design is closed to reopening on reviewer freshness alone.** A new seat executes the named unit and returns at the declared stop; **reopening requires a new material reason.**

**R28** — **three defects in R27's reporting corrected; net −8 lines.** A **fabricated FAI causal history** withdrawn *(R27 claimed swapping the materiality and adequacy axes produced "three FAI passes reached acceptance on unassessed families"; the source says "three passes went into the corpus and none into the diff" — misdirected effort, and FAI preserves Outputs 1–3 as accepted with revalidation debt, not failures)* · the **reliance instruction scoped** to governing versus evidentiary use, with the reuse half landed in `§13.2` · the **false unchanged/changed tally** removed rather than recounted · and R27's duplicated quotation recital and eight-row table compacted without losing their operative counterparts. **The lesson recorded with it: verifying several quotations does not verify the explanation written between them** — six citations checked out and the invented sentence sat in the gap between two of them.


**R27** — **integrates the recovered FAI mid-arc method lessons. This entry records what changed and its review status — it does not restate the lessons a third time.**

**Source posture:** the FAI carrier was extracted at `65f310e0…` and **hash-confirmed as blob `9835715e…`** before any citation was used; each load-bearing passage was read in place rather than taken from the review. **This changes execution obligations and is not hygiene.** The accepted **investigation design** remains R24.

**Corrected at R28 — two defects in this entry's own account, both found by review:**
- **A fabricated causal history.** R27 wrote *"swapping ② and ③ is how three FAI passes reached acceptance on unassessed families."* **The FAI source says no such thing.** Its actual sentence is *"swapping those two is how three passes went into the corpus and none into the diff"* — **misdirected effort, not three acceptance events** — and FAI separately preserves Outputs 1–3 as accepted with scoped revalidation debt, **not** as failures. **Withdrawn from both locations.** *This is the sharpest instance yet of the pattern: verifying several quotations does not verify the explanation written between them — committed in the same revision that installed the rule against it.*
- **A tally that contradicted its own table.** R27 claimed *"six retained unchanged, six now explicit."* The rows show otherwise: only `N3`, `N6` and `N10` were retained unchanged, while `N2`, `N7` and `N12` each changed. **The aggregate is removed rather than recounted** — the twelve dispositions are the record.


**R26** — **the writing seat verified the reviewer's claims itself rather than adopting them, and all of them held. Two were against R25's own text, one of them an overclaim of the kind this carrier exists to catch.**

**Verified, not accepted on assertion:** the read graph's Major-Arc Intake route **already names `v4_C2_source_base_declaration.md` and `v4_C3_C4_estate_coverage_preflight.md` by path** (L193, and again at L270/L284), and the repertoire already carries them at **`M-101`** *(source-base declaration)* and **`M-104`** *(zero-unknown coverage manifest — "exhaustive accounting, selective depth")*. **So R25's claim that ECB "was not routing it" and that the next agent "would have re-derived it or, worse, winged it" is FALSE and is withdrawn.** R25 added ECB-local convenience links to sources the shared boot already required. **Four conditions are distinct and only the last counts: reachable through shared routing · explicitly required for this assignment · actually consumed · adequately used.** R25 improved the second and claimed credit for the first.

**`§0` is corrected IN PLACE — replaced, not appended beneath itself, because appending is the failure this carrier has now recorded five times.**

**The method lineage is now named in `§0` instead of being rediscovered per agent** — read-graph Major-Arc Intake *(and its rule that the gate sequence is arc-specific, so no prior arc's shape is automatically owed)* · AWP `§2.1` for execution and replacement continuity · FAI R8 `§4`/`§§4.1`–`4.4`/`§5` for scoped method and gate-output precedent · FAI PRE-0 `§§1`/`4`/`6`–`7` for the plan audit and its closing instruction, ***"after this, stop designing the design process"*** · and the repertoire as an **optional menu conferring no execution authority**, which FAI R8 `§0` records being got wrong in both directions. **Plus the document-authority machinery behind this arc's presence-is-not-authority rule** — `00_document_governance_and_taxonomy`, `05_supersession_conflict_ledger` and the Control Plane — **named here because a fresh agent should not have to infer where classification and supersession are governed.** None of this imports FAI's agent count, gate sequence, conclusions or paused work.

**R25** — **the operator's objection was that none of this survives into the next agent, and he was right. A handoff cannot carry it, because the drifted handoff IS the failure.** Three changes, none of which touch the investigation contract; **the accepted content revision remains R24.**

**`§0` ENTRY GATE, hardwired at the top of the file.** Every agent enters through these bytes whatever else it was handed, and **its first return must carry an eight-line entry receipt or the return is not a unit result and must be redone.** The receipt cannot be produced from a summary: it demands the accepted head and blob, the activated unit quoted with confirmation an **operator** activated it, the writable sections, **the source-field accounting performed against the LIVE catalog and read graph rather than the vocabulary already in this draft**, **what was read natively versus taken on a predecessor's receipt**, the relevant families **not** assessed, the stop condition, and **one thing in this carrier the agent thinks is wrong** — because an agent that finds a 1,200-line plan flawless has probably not read it. The gate also restates the three rules most often lost in transmission: a register row or charter is never personal consumption · **verification of what you wrote is not discovery of what you omitted** · running out of context is a legitimate stop and an **illegitimate relevance judgment**.

**The estate-accounting method is routed at `§13.2`, and it already existed.** `v4_C2_source_base_declaration.md` is *"the gate that proves the full estate is loaded + named"* — a full-estate reconciliation, `CLOSED / accepted` 2026-06-13, anchored to catalog, read graph, evidence router and supersession ledger. `v4_C3_C4_estate_coverage_preflight.md` is the zero-unknown routing manifest that accounts for **every** file *"without rereading 61 planning files,"* with a declared depth rule. **This is the estate's own answer to "too large to survey."** *(**Overclaim withdrawn at R26:** R25 asserted ECB *"was not routing it"* and that the next agent *"would have re-derived it or, worse, winged it."* **Verified false** — the read graph's Major-Arc Intake route already named both by path, and the repertoire carries them at `M-101`/`M-104`. R25 added ECB-local links to already-routed sources. **Sixth instance of this carrier's overclaim pattern; second in which the writing seat claimed a discovery it had not made.**)* Carried as **method exemplars, never as ECB's coverage certificate**: their classifications were made for a different assignment, so **relevance and depth are resolved against THIS question**, and **a finite file inventory is not a knowledge denominator.**

**R24** — **a source-status correction to one routing row. The investigation contract is unchanged: no decision, hypothesis, output, coverage obligation, specification or execution rule is touched, so `RV-9`'s `ACCEPT_AS_G0_PLAN` reasoning applies to this content unaltered.**

**R23's competitive-estate row cited document line counts — *"478 lines," "573 lines"* — as if length evidenced substance.** That is the **presence-is-not-authority** error this arc exists to catch, committed in the row added to prevent under-inheritance. **Length establishes neither present authority nor completed coverage, and the counts are removed.**

**The concrete defect underneath it: the federation arc is a SPLIT OBJECT and R23 routed only half of it.** Its 2026-05-17 status update records **PARTIAL ACTIVATION to Day-0 substrate** — tied to a three-spa Day-1 deployment with plastics next — under which **the `FUTURE_ARC` document remains source of truth for the original arc and the FUTURE deferrals (Modes 3/4/5), while `doctrine/DL-21_federation_topology_DRAFT_2026-05-17.md` carries the Day-0 ACTIVATED primitives**, both maintained until Phase-1 hardening v2 lands. **An executor entering only the document R23 named would have missed the activated half.** `DL-21` is now routed and the split is stated. **Per-document status governs; this is a worked example, not a licence to assume the other rows are uniformly current.**

**Non-re-derivation is stated explicitly as NOT non-challenge.** The balance already existed — `§12.5` permits reuse only where requirement, assumptions, operating conditions, failure modes and maturity correspond · `§13.0` permits inferred and novel synthesis with no estate ancestor required · `§13.2` requires discovery beyond the account already written — but the rule now says so in the same row, because *"do not re-derive"* read alone could license treating a prior favourable verdict as exempting a changed assumption.

**Also recorded, against this seat:** the R23 return claimed *"nobody has found a design flaw since R15"* and that the remainder was *"essentially transcription."* **Withdrawn as inaccurate.** `F-01` was a completion-contract defect found at R21, and the participation-and-repeatability obligation was a substantive sharpening — neither was transcription. **The claim never entered the carrier** (verified: zero occurrences) and is corrected here so the record does not carry a self-serving account of the review's yield. Relatedly, the R23 return called *surfacing what the decomposition cannot hold* **the whole value**; that is too narrow — the plan asks both where the arrangement breaks **and** what it enables *(preparing useful work while a decision stays open · preserving options · reusing still-valid evidence · admitting a new capability without rebuilding everything · continuing care through an appropriate alternative)*.

**R23** — **completes a repair R22 reported as done, and closes the gap between "the arrangement works" and "anyone will participate in it."**

**`B-01` was applied by appending.** R22 wrote the act-specific role separation **underneath** the heading *"The three-way split that Amazon does not have,"* the assertion that *"three roles Amazon fuses are split here,"* and the Amazon/Care table fixing the purchaser to the provider and authority to the clinician bounded by payer coverage. **The section asserted a premise and then contradicted it four lines later, while `§16` recorded the premise as replaced.** R23 **deletes** them. **This is the third consecutive revision in which a repair landed in one place while the material it superseded survived elsewhere — and the second in which the receipt overstated it.** The heading is now *Who is this for? Product focus and act-specific authority*, and it carries the commercial corollary: **serving the relationship must not become an excuse for an indeterminate buyer or an interface requiring every participant to understand the whole substrate.**

**Participation and repeatability are now stated rather than assumed.** The failure prevented: an account that is technically sufficient and locally useful while resting on counterparty cooperation nobody has shown will be given. Output C must now separate technical sufficiency from incremental value against a credible whole alternative, and where value depends on participation must name **the bootstrap path before network density · the partner's existing alternative · who funds integration, qualification and ongoing operating work · and whether benefit is confined to OMNI-originated transactions or presupposes a counterparty-side product.** **Those are different systems to build** — a pharmacy accepting requests, a pharmacy adopting OMNI tooling across its book, and bridging an existing rail are not one thing called *partner participation*. Repeatability is stated too: what carries to the next deployment, what must stay local. `D6` consumes it; Output D carries what remains unproved. **Three honest results stay legitimate, including *the incumbent bundle supplies this span with less burden and OMNI should not own it.***

**The competitive estate is routed, not re-derived — and this correction came from the operator.** The reviewing seat grounded the sharpening in the residual/moat frame alone. **The estate in fact already holds hospital/EHR gravity work, a reality map, a 478-line adversarial superiority expansion, a wedge plan, a 573-line federation-permeability arc, and an argued case for ground-up federation being competitive.** All verified present at `analysis_nonbinding` maturity. **`§13.2` now carries a NON-RE-DERIVATION RULE**: enter these at their termini, take the inherited position at its actual status, record agreement, extension or contradiction — **and treat an ungroundable comparative claim as an evidence debt, not a finding.** Without it ECB would have spent the arc rebuilding positioning analysis it already owns.

**The last categorical FAI diagnosis is withdrawn.** `§12.9` no longer asserts that FAI *"reached Output 4 before it had a working method"*; it states the scoped method ECB inherits. The execution contract never depended on the diagnosis, and attributed history stays in `§16`.

**`B-01` and `B-02` are incomplete propagation, not new objections** — both corrections were made during design and did not fully leave the operative text, which is this carrier's recurring failure in its quietest form. **Stage A read those passages too charitably; the transcripts exposed them.** That is the two-stage method working as intended, and it is the argument for it.

|---|---|
| heterogeneous AI participation with differing standing is unmodelled | **Care `§9a` L270** names *"patient ± private AI · provider ± preferred external AI · OMNI-native assistant · specialist AI · pharmacy AI"* with participant/context/authority gates, represented principal, sponsor/incentive, model version, uncertainty, exposure, **`unseen` disposition**, influence, and independence-vs-shared-source |
| nothing covers a concern from a party that cannot dictate the response | **Accountability `§§4`–`5a`** separates **reporter from detector**, handles external and weak-identity reporters, and preserves a communication obligation owed to a reporter |
| nothing covers an artifact one cannot assume is complete | **Observation contract** already separates `media_artifact → observation → extracted_assertion` from adoption and records *how faithfully* it was captured; **C4.3** already carries record-versus-actuality fixtures (`note+charge 50 / actual DISPUTED`; `50 administered / 40 intended`) |

**The word-count test was the error.** *"`observer` appears zero times in 1,055 lines"* cannot establish conceptual absence — it is the search-miss-as-absence failure this carrier's own `§13.0` forbids, and I used it to justify two hypotheses. **The questions survive; their novelty framing does not.** They are now composition-and-pressure questions against inherited mechanisms, which is the harder and more useful form.

| other repair | what changed |
|---|---|
| **`H-RECORD`'s universal** | *"OMNI never receives the event"* **withdrawn** — depending on what *event* and *composed* mean it is either a truism about all digital representation or a flattening of materially different evidence. An authored operative note, a signed device message, a sampled waveform and an execution receipt have **different production processes**. Replaced with a per-use requirement: establish what the evidence represents, how produced, coverage required, what is missing, and whether it suffices **for the conclusion being drawn** |
| **omission was over-blessed** | R19 treated the tired start and the internal process lesson as legitimately omitted. **Too categorical.** Whether something must be documented, disclosed, investigated or reported turns on significance and applicable obligation, not author preference. *Not everything belongs in the operative note; that does not mean it belongs nowhere.* Clinical record · execution evidence · incident investigation · required disclosure · genuinely private reflection stay distinct |
| **`D8` offered non-selectable options** | legal duties and patient rights were listed as alternatives Nick and Knox could choose among. **They are constraints.** Implementations are compared within them; unresolved applicability needs qualified evidence. Added: a legally sufficient disclosure may still be **operationally insufficient** for the responsibility a participant proposes to accept |
| **Output C was too blunt** | R19 forbade *measuring* unobserved improvement. **Withdrawn** — observable outcome change can be measured without observing mechanism. What is forbidden is inventing causal explanation or claiming credit without basis |
| **`"owes nothing"`** | `§11.6` inferred it from the absence of a provider/OMNI contract. **Withdrawn** — an undertaking to the patient, or duty from role, law or circumstance, may apply; that is a legal question this arc cannot settle by assumption |
| **`SPEC-ORD`/`SPEC-GEN` mischaracterized** | R19's return called them *"exactly those two poles."* **They are not.** `SPEC-GEN` tests generated-intervention change **before administration**, not live supervision; the live supervisory case belongs to the adaptive-physical-activity family |

**Added:** `§6.3`'s **two-scale comparison table** — seven questions the ordinary lab→prescribe→pharmacy case and the multi-participant procedure must both answer — installed as commissioning requirement, cross-referenced from `§11.6`, **with no `Observer` class minted and no universal observer lifecycle**. Plus **five participant conditions** that must stay distinct, and the corrected robotics source at its actual scope.

**Still open and disclosed rather than papered over:** the earlier supplier-access / tenant-BYOM / local-to-shared-capability amendment could not be located by this seat in the carrier or its own context. Knox's five restated conditions are lodged at `§11.6`; **the original packet is requested, not reconstructed.**

**R19** — **operator-introduced scope, added at the acceptance boundary. SUBSTANTIVE: the Knox `RV-4` approval covered R18 and does NOT extend to this revision.** Recorded that way deliberately, because the alternative — folding new scope into an acceptance receipt under a prior approval — is the exact move `RV-4`'s own relay prohibited.

**What prompted it.** Nick raised two operating scenarios at G0: a surgical case with multiple mechanical and intelligent participants under asymmetric observation, and a provider declining to supply case material that a supplier needs to improve the instrument the provider depends on. **Both are inside the arc's stated scope. The test applied was not "is this mentioned somewhere" but "would an author following this plan be REQUIRED to confront it"** — the same standard `RV-4` used on business formation.

**Reachable as written, so nothing was added** — recorded so the additions below are not read as a larger gap than they are. A supervisor who cannot understand what an autonomous participant did is **`H-OPAQUE`**, which already forbids narration-as-explainability and names the attested-claim shape. Attributing a fault across human, robot and device participants is **`D4`** plus duty-chain scoping. The tri-party undertaking question is **`D1`**. And the *"how is a surgical suite the same as a peptide prescription to a 503A pharmacy"* question is already the arc's spine: **`SPEC-ORD` and `SPEC-GEN` are exactly those two poles**, and the generalization between them is what `§12.9.5` exists to test.

| addition | why it was not reachable |
|---|---|
| **`H-RECORD`** — OMNI never receives the event; it receives an artifact **composed by a participant about** the event, with principled omission as a legitimate feature, and **authoring competence varying independently of performance competence**. Machine participants have the same structure | The carrier had provenance, custody, acknowledgment-versus-custody and attestation — **all of which presume the artifact represents the event.** `H-OPAQUE` covers an assertion one cannot verify; **nothing covered an artifact one cannot assume is complete.** If it holds, every mechanism resting on reused evidence rests on artifacts whose fidelity is a variable |
| **`H-STAND`** — detection is asymmetric across participants with different standing; whether observing creates standing, or standing creates duty, is unmodelled | **`observer` appeared zero times in 1,055 lines.** `H-IMPACT` covers *unobserved*; nothing covered *observed by a party with different standing* — including a patient-retained reviewer with no contract to provider or OMNI |
| **`D8`** — what must flow between participants as **obligation** versus what is negotiated as **exchange**, over the same event | The operative record is simultaneously a clinical/legal entitlement and commercially valuable material. **No row owned the boundary between the required floor and the negotiated zone**, nor what happens when a counterparty supplies only the floor |
| **Output C observability bound** | The value thesis could otherwise claim improvement it cannot see. The operator's private resolution and the supplier's internal process fix are **causally load-bearing and legitimately invisible.** OMNI may not claim credit for what it cannot observe, nor treat unobserved learning as absent |
| **`§11.6` asymmetric-detection requirement** | Makes `H-STAND`/`H-RECORD` an execution obligation rather than a hypothesis an author may pass by |

**Deliberately NOT added, to protect the repaired coverage contract:** no breaker family, no regime, no axis. **`§12.4`'s arithmetic is untouched at 48 + 6 + 6 + 2.** The asymmetric-detection obligation rides inside the existing individual regime.

**Nothing here decides an answer.** Each addition is a question with a falsifier that can kill it, and `H-STAND` explicitly admits *"standing should not follow observation"* as a legitimate result. **One external item is carried at its actual scope, attributed and NOT promoted** (**correcting R20's "owed if this is ever to bind anything" — that was wrong.** `GRD-037` governs **filing and indexing at capture**, which is owed **now** and is **UNMET**; `GRD-036` governs **promotion**, which is a separate later gate. This transaction carries no authorization for `ingestion/` writes, so the unmet prerequisite is **recorded, not discharged** — and no evidence campaign is opened to decorate this amendment). *TechCrunch, 2026-09-11:* a **possible** Sequoia-led Mecka financing near a $500M valuation, **terms not final**. Mecka's own description presents data collection, annotation, quality assurance, evaluation and deployment as connected work and states **raw video alone is insufficient** — **the company's claims about its own offering, not evidence that any surgical capability works.** **The inference `D8` should test, not assume:** the valuable learning material may be the **governed linkage among context, action, configuration, outcome and correction** rather than ownership of a video file — which if true is an advantage OMNI is positioned to produce **and** a responsibility and cost it would carry. **Access to clinically meaningful context confers no right to commercialize it.**

**One earlier external item was checked and is NOT carried:** the supplied TechCrunch link (OpenAI pausing Pro signups under Astra demand) concerns subscription and infrastructure capacity, **not** robotics training-data acquisition; the operator's underlying point stands independently and needs no such citation, so no evidence row is proposed and the evidence router was not invoked.

**R16** — **the integrated correction from two commissioned, independently frozen G0 reviews. Both returned `PLAN_SURVIVES_WITH_EXACT_AMENDMENTS`; neither found a defect requiring a different arc shape, and neither found the plan overbuilt.** Fourteen findings adopted, two narrowed, none rejected. **Two of the repairs make the plan smaller** (one coverage definition replacing three; a withdrawn falsifier). **Three of the adopted defects sat inside the machinery R13–R15 added to prevent exactly this class of failure** — the snapshot controls, the specs prepared to prove usability, and the first unit's own source route. That is the eighth through tenth instances of this carrier's catalogued failure class (*recovering part of a prior instruction, dropping its qualification, presenting restoration as a breakthrough*), and it is recorded rather than smoothed over.

**Verification posture for this revision.** The writing seat independently verified the five load-bearing claims before adopting them, rather than accepting either report on its authority: the `_04` → `_02 §15` derivation order (confirmed — `_04` L13, *"Opus's integration lives in `_02 §15`"*); the total absence of `_01`, `_03` and `_00` from all 936 lines (confirmed — zero occurrences); the dropped `exposes` relation (confirmed — one occurrence in the carrier, in an unrelated rule); the AI-authority slogan's presence (confirmed); and Agent Work Protocol L168's prohibition on chat-recovered bases (confirmed verbatim). **The remaining findings were adopted on the reviewers' cited evidence and are marked as such below.**

**Two things the reviews establish that are worth more than the repairs.** First, `§7`'s characterisation of the inherited candidate was **independently verified against native bytes on five load-bearing elements and found accurate** — retiring a live provisional flag at zero cost. Second, both seats tested and confirmed that the evaluator design carries all three required result capabilities, including **the hardest one: reporting that a result cannot be decided.** The defects were in two instances, not in the design.

**R15** — **four corrections, one of them against R14's own headline claim.**

**The discovery attribution was wrong, and wrong in the exact way it claimed to fix.** R14 quoted FAI `§E15.17.8` as *"a read is triggered by an exact retained claim, not by breadth"* and built a causal story on it. **The sentence does not end there.** It continues: *"— most often a retained law whose only stated support is a source this output has not opened; also a relationship or contradiction surfaced during recovery, **a source likely to expose a missing claim rather than support an existing one**, a reviewing-seat challenge, or operator direction."* Agent Work Protocol durable fact 5 carries the same capability independently — *"required source floor **and** open inquiry aperture (a floor is a minimum, never a boundary)"* (L111). **Requirement discovery was already in the inherited method, twice. ECB had inherited it incompletely.** So: **the explicit two-trigger rule stays** — being explicit in ECB is worth it — **but it is a restored qualification, not an invention, and it does not explain the arc's setbacks.** *Recovering part of a prior instruction, dropping its qualification, then presenting restoration as a breakthrough is the failure this seat has now committed six times — this instance inside the revision that claimed to cure it.*

**`SPEC-ORD`'s positive path let prescriber adoption stand in for the counterparty's commitment** — the precise substitution its own `P1` forbids. Repaired: on adoption the demonstrated path ends at **an authorized replacement request**, because `Y`'s substantive acceptance and performance evidence are separately owned and were never supplied by the sequence; completion requires extending it with that evidence. On decline, the positive path is resolution without the substitution. **A candidate that completes on the prescriber's signature alone now fails.**

**`SPEC-GEN`'s `P5` routed reconsideration to "the authority named at `T1`"** — historical identity as present authorization, which is `H-SURV`'s failure inside the spec meant to test it. Repaired to the **currently rightful authority for the affected decision**, with `T1` preserved as historical evidence; the holder may have departed, lost authority or transferred it.

**`§12.9.7a` added — coordination and snapshot controls, referenced rather than restated.** Names `agent_work_protocol.md` `§2.1` (one active writer per writable object and per branch · explicit replacement transfer · the **Coordination Overlay** at L131 · conditional base/reference mechanics) and `operator_context_and_collaboration_model.md` `§§2.6`–`2.7` (review-object identity, diff receipts, relay-recipient check). States ECB's default once — one carrier writer, snapshot review, explicit replacement — and requires a declaration of question, owner, input, owned output, writable boundary, dependency and disposition path **before** any additional durable contribution is activated. **Concurrency alone creates no parent package, common base or integrator.** The writer is identified by **instance and bounded work item, not the generic name "Opus."**

**And the head-versus-content rule is restored.** It existed in R2's `§2.1` receipt and was **lost in R5's compression** — the exact convention the operator remembers building. **A frozen object is verified by its content blob, never by branch-head equality**; the FAI pause handoff is the worked example, three recorded heads against one carrier blob `9835715e`. ***"The branch moved" and "the thing we reviewed changed" are different statements and neither establishes the other.***

**One overclaim withdrawn:** these are **manual controls, not semantic enforcement.** A missing insight can leave a perfectly tidy diff, and no convention here detects a weak agent automatically.

**R14** `3a9b80ec` — **three verified defects fixed; one item declined.**

**Fixed — the discovery trigger, which is the mechanism behind the operator having to supply breadth.** `§13.2` inherited FAI's *"a read is triggered by an exact retained claim, not by breadth"* verbatim. Alone, that protects claims already written and protects nothing against an **omitted requirement** — an agent can conclude nothing material remains because every sentence it chose to write carries a citation. **Now two triggers: claim verification AND requirement discovery**, with the completion claim requiring both halves — no known material gap behind a retained claim, *and* the discovery work performed to look beyond the account, with what it found.

**Fixed — the two specifications were instructions for writing specifications, not specifications.** Fifth overclaim of this kind by this seat. `SPEC-ORD` and `SPEC-GEN` now carry declared inputs, stepwise event sequences with timestamps, expected predicates each with provenance, positive paths, must-fail counterexamples and limits — including the two **decidable rules** that stop an evaluator inventing a convenient reading mid-trace: same source event identity ⇒ deduplicate, and a declared prerequisite set deciding whether new evidence invalidates or merely triggers review. **Still unscored.**

**Fixed — the `§15.5` entry route led around `§12.9`**, the contract a replacement most needs. It now enters there first.

**Added, small:** Output B carries the **coverage argument** and what independent challenge exposed — *"held-out cases supplied"* is an activity, *"the generalization survived within these limits"* is a result. Every accepted conclusion carries a **result type** (settled · conditional · blocking semantic question · later empirical proof), and **a named owner prevents abandonment but does not supply a missing answer** — an unresolved dependency may not stand in for an unanswered part of the operating account.

**Declined:** a new eight-row table of analytical questions. `§§9`–`§11` already carry those distinctions, `§13.0` forbids a competing taxonomy, and the review itself said the repair was to make the existing questions select the evidence — which is what the Output-B change does. **Adding the table would have been the fourth vocabulary in one carrier.**

**R13** `6f1321f6` — **installs the execution contract.** R12's I1/I2/I3 are closed and not reopened. The question, corrected premises, decisions, three phases, Outputs A–D and boundaries are retained. **Nothing in G1 was performed and no architectural verdict was populated.**

**`§12.9` added — the contract binds every agent, not a kickoff prompt.** The failure it answers is the one the operator named: FAI reached Output 4 before it had a working method **(the accompanying *"roughly a quarter"* quantification is withdrawn as unsupported at R22 — historical record of what was claimed, not a standing finding)**, and a G1-wide control written inside Output 4 would have been invisible to Output 5. **`§12.9.1`** gives a successor six entry facts with a durable home each, separates the **accepted baseline** from the working carrier, makes reading proportional to context freshness, holds each agent to reading the natives for claims **it personally asserts** (Agent Work Protocol `§2.1`, verified at L66/L105 — eight durable facts; replacement assumes the same lane after explicit transfer and freshness check), and states the stale-`main` exception once so it is not rediscovered every entry.

**`§12.9.2` — a phase label is not authority to finish the phase.** Work units name one question, a source packet, the sections they may update, an expected result and a review stop; they end on the result **or earlier at a semantic-load checkpoint**. **`§12.9.3` specifies the first unit now** — the inherited joint care/business account, entering through `_07` as the consumption endpoint with `_02 §15` operative and `_04` adjudicating, writing to `§7.4` and `§13.2`, stopping before the next cohort. **Not the phase, and not an instruction to rebuild `EVRUN-000012`.**

**`§12.9.4` — what running a test means**, so nobody designs the method mid-arc: default is a source-grounded stepwise design trace; **prose execution, simulation, implementation and deployed proof are never reported as one thing**; seven required elements per scored case; **the evaluator may not repair an underspecified candidate mid-trace and score the repair**; reviewer agreement establishes no efficacy, no control-system safety and no institutional acceptance; comparison holds the demanded outcome and operating span constant; value counts work shifted onto patients and counterparties. **`§12.9.5`** prepares **SPEC-ORD** (delayed acceptance, duplicated acknowledgment, proposed substitution — receipt ≠ acceptance ≠ fulfillment ≠ adopted clinical change) and **SPEC-GEN** (irreversible preparation, then a material context change — manufacturing completion ≠ current suitability, consumed material accounted, no assumption that physical work rolls back), **both deliberately unscored.**

**`§12.9.6` gives every output a home** — `§7.4` account · `§11.6` specs and results · Output C · Output D · `§13.2` source control · `§15.4` remaining work · `§16` lineage only. **A correction enters its section before its unit is returned; *"discussed with Nick"* is not a preservation state.** **`§12.9.7`** sets replacement readiness with the **interrupted-unit case** a successor must survive, distinguishes an author self-check from a cold-agent test, and adopts Output-4's four review responsibilities as behaviour rather than headcount.

**`§13.2` upgraded from a reading list to a live working control** on FAI `§E15.17.8`'s pattern (verified at carrier L6015): five columns, maintained **as reads occur**, answerable at any moment; **no row terminates at "read" and no row owes an empty field**; depth follows consequence; a read is triggered by an exact retained claim, not by breadth; a new source mid-unit resets nothing; **no coverage percentage and no closed denominator** — the only available completion claim is *no known material source gap behind a retained claim*. A **decision-territory coverage map** forces the floor past the sources whose vocabulary already matches the hypotheses.

**Two floor corrections.** The Care evidence ledger was carried as a **single row against 566**; it now requires the accepted dispositions and outstanding corrections bearing on every retained Care claim, and **`HANDOFF_2026-07-13_care_forensic_inheritance_audit.md` is added in full** — Lanes 1–6 accepted, its method binding on any Care read, including *chronology proves lineage not authority* and *subagents locate, the parent reads every verdict-changing source*. **Reading the capture while skipping the accepted adjudication of that capture is still under-inheritance.** And **C4.6 Rx L2** is added as the later reviewed engineering/conformance object through which external-counterparty claims route — not a parent charter, not the residual/moat document.

**`§11.6` added** — six required scenario families as conditional pressures rather than vendor predictions, plus the **extension test** (introduce a materially different participant *after* the account is formed; *"everything fits"* is not the required answer) and the **credible competing whole arrangement** rule. **`§1` gains an operational-state block** so a successor reads active writer, phase, bounded work item and next permitted action from the carrier.

**One boundary held against the operator's wording:** the plan does **not** promise that no new test will be designed after kickoff — that would reward failing to discover anything. What is settled before kickoff is the **method, initial specifications, coverage obligations, result meanings, revision rules and decision authority**; a later case instantiates or extends that method for an actual finding rather than reopening what a test is.

**R12** `3c84cf9e` — **bounded integrity correction on a pinned entry object** (head `b859131b`, blob `d56cc398`, verified before editing). Three corrections, no reopening of R11's substantive repairs.

**I1 — version binding.** `§15.0`'s live acceptance checklist still asked Nick and Knox to **accept R10 inside an R11 document**, with a matching *"Produced by R10"* and a stop condition naming R10 as the next candidate. **Acceptance now binds to the reviewed content revision identified by head and primary blob**, not to a revision name, so a later patch cannot inherit an earlier revision's acceptance. Both boxes unchecked; no activation recorded.

**I2 — source and section identity.** The CNS row cited **`§7.6` as though it were a CNS-contract section; it is a thesis v2 provenance pointer.** Corrected to the contract's own `§2` (governing formulation), `§3` (scope model), `§7` (cross-operator coordination), `§9.1` (layered-context-packet rule). The integrated-thesis cell expanded to its full path.

**R10** `e835deab` — **plan repair by the incoming seat. Operative premises corrected in place; execution method inherited rather than reinvented; the arc bounded by the decisions it must produce.**

*Method inherited:* **FAI R8 `§§4.1–4.3`** adopted at `§13.0` as identified method inheritance at control ref `65f310e0` — whole-source default with admissible/inadmissible bases for reading less · claim authority is the layer it appears in, and correcting a citation does not correct the conclusion it carried · unresolved semantic load as the control variable with a synthesis checkpoint and a six-field record before retaining an architecture-changing finding · the five distinct acts and the promotion hold · entry at a workstream's current terminus, with *frozen* ≠ unreadable · ten dispositions · basis honesty · the **choice-versus-fact** rule that settles who may decide what in `§12.7` · the six operativity questions. **Not permission to resume FAI, import its conclusions, or activate PR #19.**

**R7** `30defca4` — **seven R6 overreaches withdrawn; three of the operator's questions answered structurally.** *Withdrawn:* held commitments as the **universal** speed mechanism (bounded at `§3.2a` to contention over scarce excludable resources — **you cannot reserve a blood pressure or a biological response**, a reservation can itself lapse, and an idempotency key is not an inventory reservation) · *"a copy of state is exactly the wrong shape"* (contradicted this carrier's own rule; data, live stream, execution contract and authority grant do **four different jobs** and must not substitute — plus the missed requirement that **one packet timestamp must not imply simultaneous observation**) · **arbitration of rights over physical variables as a general model** (mutual exclusion is one mechanism beside coordinated simultaneous action, sequencing, inhibition, supervision, shared constraint and reconfiguration; a qualified authority must choose another accepted mode, human-directed mode, different participant, delay, transfer, bounded emergency response — or refuse to begin). **Absence claim corrected:** the `capability_envelope` / `delegated_authority_envelope` family exists and thesis **v3 §B** reportedly already names the *Capability Envelope Contract* and *Context Packet* deliverables — **never read by this author**;
